16
tamiḻ ārāycci
muṉṉuri
tamiḻippaṟṟiya ārāycci nūlkaḷ mikaccilavē veḷivan tuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷuṭ perumpālāṉa vaṭamoḻiyiyē tamatu tāy moḻiyeṉa apimāṉikkiṉṟavarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭavi. ākavē avikaḷuḷ nām tamiḻippaṟṟiya uṇmi varalāṟukaḷik kāṇutal aritākiṉṟatu. ikkaruttiṉiyē intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi ārāynta ayalnāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ mēlum mēlum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻ ārāycci eṉpatu; palkalik kaḻakattilō, vēṟu ārāyccik kaḻakattilō, patavi ēṟṟu vēlipuriyum paṭṭatārikaḷ cilarāl māttiram ceyyattakkatu; ēṉiyar ataṉic ceyyum āṟṟal cālātavarkaḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa karuttukkaḷum nilavukiṉṟaṉa. iṉit tamiḻ ārāycciyil aṟiñar palar, nuḻiyāmikkup piṟa kāraṇamu muṇṭu. ittuṟiyil varunti uḻippavarkaḷukkup pōtiya ūtiyam kiṭippatilli. tamiḻ vaḷarccikkākavē irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉac collik koḷḷum niliyaṅkaḷum avarkaḷukku utaviyaḷittu ūkkam kāṭṭuvatilli. tamiḻ aṟivucāṉṟa tiru. kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi, M.A.M.L. avar kaḷukkē cila puttaka veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷ oru pakkattukku oru rūpāy vītam ūtiyam aḷittu nūlkaḷiṉ patippurimiyip peṟṟārkaḷāṉāl, cātāraṇa ārāycciyāḷariṉ nilimi eṉṉa eṉpatic caṟṟē cintittup pāruṅkaḷ. iṉṟu tamiḻ ārāycci eṉpatu ōraḷavil araciya lōṭu toṭarpu paṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻar tammiyum tamatu moḻiyiyum paṟṟiya ellā uṇmi varalāṟukaḷiyum paṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭi yirukkiṉṟatu. ākavē potu makkaḷ uṇmiyi aṟiyak kūṭiya muṟi yil innūl veḷivarukiṉṟatu.
ceṉṉi
21.3.1947 na.ci. kantiyā
tamiḻ ārāycci
tōṟṟuvāy
tamiḻippaṟṟiyum, tamiḻarippaṟṟiyum tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ paraviyuḷḷaṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇam, tamiḻ camakkirutattiṉ vaḻimoḻi eṉṟum, tamiḻariṉ nākarikam, vaṭa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum teṉ ṉāṭṭiṟku vanta pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉarāṟ kaṭṭi eḻuppappaṭṭatu eṉṟum palar eḻuti vantamiyēyākum. ārāycciyil ik karuttukkaḷ muṟṟum tavaṟuṭiyaṉa. iṉṟu ik karuttukkaḷi eṭuttu āḷuvōr, uṇmi aṟiyamāṭṭātu paḻam pōkkiṉar eṉa otukkappaṭuvar. tamiḻariyum tamiḻiyum paṟṟiya varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷa ikkālat til āriyattāl tamiḻ vaḷam aṭintatu; āriyap pirāmaṇarāl tamiḻar nākarikam aṭintārkaḷ eṉa nāṭṭa oruvar muṉ varuvārēl, avar pūmi uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉataṉṟu; taṭṭi vaṭiviṉatē eṉa nāṭṭuvataṟku muṉ vantavarāvar. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ārāycci, tiruvaḷḷuvar ārāycci eṉat tamiḻar perumikaḷi nāṭṭuvaṉapōṉṟa kaṭṭurikaḷiyō ciṟu nūlkaḷiyō eḻutum ciṟ cilar, tamiḻiṉ ākkattukkum tamiḻariṉ uyarvukkum vaṭamoḻiyum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarumē kāraṇam eṉak kuṟippākavō, veḷippaṭiyākavō tōṉṟumpaṭi eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar. an nūlkaḷip payilum nammavar palar uṇmiyaṟiyātu peritum taṭumāṟukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmiyil tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ākkam aḷittavarkaḷ āriyarēyāyiṉ, avvāṟu kūṟuvatil iḻukku yātum illi. ārāycciyil āriyarukku nākarikattiyum, avar moḻikku eḻuttiyum, avarkaḷ payilvataṟkup pala nūlkaḷiyum ceytu aḷittavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷāyiruppati varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ teḷivāka ārāyntu kūṟiyirukka, avikaḷip payiṉṟu uṇmi teḷiyātu eḻuti varum ciṟa cilar karuttukkaḷiṉ ovvāmiyit teḷivuṟutta vēṇṭi, icciṟu nūl veḷivaruvatu avaciyamākiṉṟatu.
tirāviṭam
intiya nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷuḷ mika mikap paḻami yuṭiyatu tamiḻ. vaṭakkē imayamaliyum, teṟkē kaṉṉiyākumariyum, kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭalumākiya ellikaḷuk kuṭpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷuḷ oru kāl tamiḻmoḻi oṉṟē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu1. āriyar varukikkuppiṉ vaṭakkē vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ, pala vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum ataṉ citivukaḷiyum ēṟṟup pala moḻikaḷāka māṟutalaṭintatu. ammoḻikaḷ āriyattukku iṉamuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl uṇmi ataṟku nērmāṟāṉatu.
1teṟkē vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ uṟaḻntum piṟaḻntum kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, tuḷu, maliyāḷam eṉṉum moḻikaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. immoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil āriyaccoṟkaḷi ammoḻikaḷil cērttu vaḻaṅkuvataṟkut taṉippaṭṭa vitikaḷ uṇṭu. itaṉāl ammoḻikaḷiṉ, toṭakkattil āriyac coṟkaḷ irukkavilli yeṉṉum. piṟkālattil avi cērkkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, maliyāḷam, tamiḻ eṉṉum moḻikaḷ aṭaṅkiya kūṭṭattukku moḻi nūlār tirāviṭam eṉap peyariṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyar, muṉ pūmi cāttiram campantamākat teṉṉāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟum, tiri iṭam eṉappaṭṭatē tirāviṭam eṉat tirintateṉṟum cermaṉ āciriyar oruvar ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
vaṭintiya moḻikaḷ, amippil tirāviṭamāyiruppataṟkuk kāraṇam
intiya nāṭṭi aṭinta āriyariṉ toki aṅku vāḻntu koṇṭirunta makkaḷiviṭac ciṟu tokiyākum. 2ākavē avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi aṅku vāḻntu koṇṭirunta makkaḷiṉ moḻi amippil māṟṟam uṇṭākka muṭiyavilli. āṉāl āriyac coṟkaḷ pala kalantu ammoḻiyit tiripaṭiyacceytaṉa. pala tirāviṭaccoṟ kaḷum āriya moḻiyil ceṉṟu ēṟiṉa. ṭākṭar kiṟṟal eṉpār (Dr. Kittel) tamatu kaṉṉaṭa akarātiyiṉ muṉṉuriyil vaṭamoḻi, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiliruntu iraval peṟṟateṉat karutappaṭum 420 coṟkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkuvaṉavum āriyattuk kiṉamuṭiyavi eṉṟu karutappaṭuvaṉavumākiya moḻikaḷ, amippil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi ottaṉa eṉa, pi.ti. srīṉivāca aiyaṅkār avarkaḷum tamatu intiyāviṉ kaṟkālam, tamiḻar varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlkaḷil viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇam orōriṭattut tamiḻ ilakkaṇatti ottiruttaliṉ kāraṇam
āriyar intiya nāṭṭi aṭintapōtu avarkaḷiṉ moḻi mikat tiruntamaṟṟatākaviruntatu. mikap paḻiya pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭum āriyac coṟkaḷ camakkirutattiṉum pārkkak kirēkka moḻippōkkākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu māk° mūlar kūṟiyirukkiṟār. āriyar anākarikarāka varumpōtu nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntu koṇṭirunta tirāviṭa makkaḷ, avarkaḷukku eḻuttukkaḷi utaviyum, avar moḻiyit tiruttiyamittum tuṇipurintārkaḷ. ākavē vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ pala tamiḻ uccarippi aṭintatōṭu ataṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ cilavum tamiḻ ilakkaṇatti ottaṉavāyiṉa. 1ivvuṇmiyi aṟiya māṭṭāta ārāycciyāḷar, tamiḻar āriyariṭattiliruntu cila ilakkaṇa vitikaḷi iraval peṟṟārkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvārāyiṉar. (oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ māṟalām; ataṉ ilakkaṇattit taṉippaṭṭa cilar māṟṟa muṭiyāteṉa moḻi ārāycci vallār2 kūṟuvar).
tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭāl avi vaṭamoḻikkuriyaṉavā?
oru kūṭṭattiṉar vaṭamoḻi kaṭavuḷ moḻi eṉṟum, atu piṟamoḻiyiliruntu coṟkaḷi iravalpeṟa māṭṭāteṉṟum nampikkiyuṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē ak kūṭṭattiṉar tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭum ceṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉavāyiṉ avi vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟē kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻi pala, muṇṭā moḻic coṟkaḷiyum, tirāviṭamoḻic ceṟkaḷiyum iraval peṟṟirukkiṉṟateṉpati ārāycciyaḷar kuṟippiṭṭuḷ ḷārkaḷ. piṟi°lu°ki eṉpar muṇṭā moḻic coṟkaḷ pala vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukuntiruppatik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. kaṉṉaṭa akarātiyit tokutta “ṭākṭar” kiṟṟal (Dr.Kittel) vaṭamoḻi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiliruntu iraval peṟṟateṉak karutappaṭum 420 coṟkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷār. kālṭvel pātiriyār vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟuḷḷa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tamiḻilum, āriyattilum kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷ intu-cermaṉ moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō eṉṟu pārttal vēṇṭum. avi ammoḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉ avi āriya moḻikkuriyaṉavē. intu-cermaṉ moḻikaḷukkum intu āriya moḻikkum potu vallāta piṟa coṟkaḷ intiya moḻikaḷi liruntu āriyattiṟ ceṉṟēṟiyuḷḷaṉavākum.iṉṟu nām āriyac coṟkaḷ eṉak karutuvaṉa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ eṉa aṟiyavarukiṉṟaṉa.1 āriyar mattiya veppaniliyuṭiya nāṭukaḷiliruntu intiya nāṭṭi aṭintārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya nāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭāta marañceṭi kaḷum, vilaṅku paṟavikaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum intiya nāṭṭiluḷḷaṉa. ākavē avarkaḷ apporuḷkaḷik kuṟikka intiya makkaḷ payaṉ paṭuttiya peyarkaḷiyē āṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
intiyar nākarikam eṉpatu tirāviṭar nākarikamē
iṉṟu intiya nākarikameṉṟu ciṟappākak kūṟappaṭuvataṟkuriya pakutikaḷ tirāviṭaruṭiyaṉavē; ivaṟṟi varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ naṉku ārāyntu nāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ2
1“kiṟittu piṟappataṟku muṉṉum āriyar varukikku muṉṉum tirāviṭa nākarikam, poruḷātāra, ilakkiya, tirumaṇa muṟikaḷil naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟṟiruntaṉaveṉak koḷvatil yātum mayakkam illi.”
ṭākṭar. ē.kē. āṉantak kumāracāmi
2āriya nākarikam peritum tirāviṭa nākarikac cārpu peṟṟa teṉpatil ciṟitum cantēkam illi!! - pērāciriyar rāpcaṉ
3intiya nākarikattil āriyarallāta makkaḷuṭiya nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭi peritum uḷḷateṉpatic cilvaṉ levi, piṟi°lu°ki (Sylvann Levi and Jean Prsyluski) eṉpōr teḷḷitil viḷakki eḻutiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷataṉ āṅkila moḻi peyarppik kākci (P.Cagci) eṉpavar tāmeḻutiya āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷ eṉṉum nūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. intiya varalāṟṟil, āriyarallāta makkaḷiṉ nākarika aṭippaṭi uḷḷateṉpati ellā āciriyarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ṭākṭar. e°.kē. caṭṭēci
4ār.ṭi.paṉērjiyum kāṭṭiyavāṟu arappā mokañcatarō nākarikam āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭatum, āriyarallāta makkaḷuṭiyatum ākum. palucci°tāṉattil pirākūy makkaḷ kāṇappaṭutalik koṇṭu annākarikattik kaṭṭi eḻuppiyavarkaḷ paḻiya tirāviṭarkaḷāvar eṉat tuṇiyappaṭukiṟatu- mēṟpaṭi
5“tirāviṭa nākarikatti nām oru pōtum kuṟivākak karututal kūṭātu. tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭiyiṉāl āriya nākarikam ceḻippum āḻamum eytiṟṟu. tirāviṭar pala kalikaḷil tiṟami aṭintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pāṭavum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amikkavum vallavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar” - tākūr.
camakkirutam tamiḻiṉ vēṟupāṭu
camakkirutam eṉpataṟku naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. camakkirutattukku vaṭa tamiḻ eṉpatu iṉṉoru peyar. vaṭakkē atika pirākirutac coṟkaḷi ēṟṟu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻē naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭuc camakkirutam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟateṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. caṭṭampi cāmi eṉṉum ciṟanta ārāycci aṟiñar, camakkirutat tukku aṭippaṭi tamiḻē eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār6.
“kālañceṉṟa caṭṭampi cāmikaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttup pirati oṉṟi yāṉ pārttuḷḷēṉ. caṭṭampi cāmi avarkaḷ, kalviyāḷarkaḷ viyakkakkūṭiya paranta aṟivum ārāycciyum uṭiyarāy viḷaṅkiṉār. camakkirutam eṉpataṟku naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. etiliruntu atu naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉa avar kēṭkiṉṟār. avar pala vakikaḷāl camakkirutattukku aṭippaṭi tamiḻ eṉa ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. teḷivākak kūṟumiṭattu ataṉ aṭippaṭi paḻantamiḻ” -irāma varmā ārāyccik kaka veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻ ārāycci, vaṭamoḻi ārāycciyāka māṟiyami
mēlnāṭṭu makkaḷ vantu moḻikaḷippaṟṟi ārāyntu uṇmi kāṇuvataṉ muṉpu camakkirutattiliruntē ellā moḻikaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa; ammoḻiyil uḷḷa nūlkaḷil ellāk kalikaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṉum mūṭakkoḷki makkaḷiṭiyē paraviyiruntatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil oru kūṭṭattiṉar camakkirutam tamatu tāy moḻiyeṉak karuti vantārkaḷ; varukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ, ārāycci celkiṉṟa vaḻiyil cella māṭṭārkaḷ; ārāycci evvāṟintālum camakkirutattiliruntu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa; āriya makkaḷ tirāviṭaric cīrtiruttiṉar eṉṉum paḻam piṭiyic ciṟitum kiviṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivvaki maṉappāṉmi yuṭiyavarkaḷē perumpālum tamiḻariyum tamiḻiyum paṟṟi ārāyntu nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉāl tamiḻ ārāycci ceyvōr uṇmi kāṇamāṭṭātavarkaḷākiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻiyē tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷa cilarum, tamiḻ, vaṭamoḻiyiṉāl ēṟṟamaṭintatu eṉa eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar. aṉṉavar aṟiyamikku yām iraṅka vēṇṭiyataṉṟi avarkaḷik kuṟi kūṟuvatāṟ payaṉilli.
tamiḻ ārāycci ceyvatu eppaṭi?
tamiḻ ārāycci ceyvataṉ muṉ tamiḻ, āriyam eṉṉum moḻi kaḷuḷ emmoḻi muṟpaṭṭatu? āriyar intiya nāṭṭi aṭivataṉ muṉ, āriyar tamiḻar eṉṉum iru makkaḷiṉ nākarika nili evvaki yiṉatu? anākarika maṭintirunta makkaḷiṭamiruntu nākarika makkaḷ cilavaṟṟi iraval peṟuvārkaḷā? peruntokiyāṉa makkaḷiṭiyē ciṟu kūṭṭattiṉar kalakka nērntāl emmakkaḷiṉ koḷkikaḷ karut tukkaḷ mēlōṅkum eṉpavi pōṉṟavikaḷi ārāyntu teḷital vēṇṭum. appoḻutē ārāyccikkaṇ tiṟakkum.
mayil, kaḻakam eṉpavi vaṭacoṟkaḷā?
aṇmiyil ārāycciyāḷar oruvar 1mayil, kaḻakam mutaliya coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avi vaṭamoḻikkē uriyaṉa eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. avvaṟiñar tamiḻ ārāyccit tuṟiyil nīṇṭakālam iruntavarāyiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyil pala tirāviṭar coṟkaḷ ceṉṟu ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatic ciṟitum aṟiyātu pōṉami mikavum varuntattakka toṉṟākum. itu āḻntu ārāyavēṇṭiya toṉṟu aṉṟāyiṉum, itu kāraṇamākat tamiḻ māṇavar uḷḷattil tōṉṟum mayakkattip pōkkavēṇṭiyē itaṉi īṇṭu ārāya vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
āriyamakkaḷ mattiya āciyāviṉiṉṟum vantavarkaḷ āvar. avar nāṭukaḷil mayil illi. mayil intiya nāṭṭukku uriya paṟavi. āriya makkaḷ tām muṉṉaṟiyātirunta marañceṭikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ paṟavikaḷi yum piṟavaṟṟiyum uṇartta intiya moḻic coṟkaḷiyē peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉap piṟi°lu°ki pōṉṟa ārāycci aṟiñar naṉku ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. intiyanāṭṭup paṟavikku intiyamoḻi kaḷil peyar iruntatu. vaṭamoḻiyiṟceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar kiṟṟel eṉpavarāl kāṭṭappaṭṭa pala coṟkaḷuḷ mayūram oṉṟākum.* mayil eṉṉum collē vaṭamoḻiyil mayūramākat tirintu vaḻaṅki yirukkalām. vētapāṭalkaḷil mayil irukkumāṉāl atu eppaṭi vaṭamoḻikkuriyatākum. vēta moḻiyē uccarippu muṟiyil tirāviṭac cāyal peṟṟiruntateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vētakāla muṉivarkaḷ palar tirāviṭar eṉṟu “mu°lim kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa intiyā” eṉṉum nūlāciriyarākiya iraṅkāccāriyar pōṉṟa aṟiñar kūṟiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. mayilē illāta oru nāṭṭiliruntu vanta makkaḷ, mayilik kuṟikka oru peyarik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ eṉṟum, appeyarit tamiḻ makkaḷ avarkaḷiṭamiruntu iraval peṟṟu ap paṟavikkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟutal ārāyccik kuṟiyēyākum. icciṟiya niyāyattip pērāciriyar oruvar aṟintu koḷḷātatu varuntattakkatē yākum. mayil eṉṉum col maṟṟiya āriya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatā? intiya... āriya moḻiyil māttiram atu kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ, ammoḻikku atu evvāṟu kiṭittatu eṉpatupōṉṟa ārāycci ceyvataṉṟō ārāyum muṟiyākum.
kaḻakam eṉṉum collum vaṭamoḻikkuriyateṉa mēṟpaṭi āciriyar kūṟuvārāyiṉar. tamiḻil kaḷam eṉṉum col irukkumpōtu, kaḻakam eṉṉum col kaḷam eṉpatiliruntu vantateṉṟu ēṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu. coṟkaḷ uccarippu muṟiyil ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭu vevvēṟu karuttukkaḷi viḷakkuvatu ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāṉa iyalpaṉṟō. “vē” vētamāka māṭṭātu “vit” vētamākum eṉa oru cārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉi ottatē kaḻakam vaṭamoḻi eṉak kūṟutalumākum, paḻiya āriya moḻikaḷil kaḻakam eṉṉum col uṇṭā? kāliṉ° eṉṉum aṟiñar, āriyac coṟkaḷ eṉṟu potumakkaḷum paṇṭitarum karutum coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟi ārāyntu avi tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ eṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭarallarā?
avvārāycciyāḷar mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭar eṉpa taṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ illiyeṉṟum, hera°pātiriyār, mokañcarō muttirikaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷi vācittu, avvācippiṉ mūlam aṅku vāḻntavarkaḷ tirāviṭar eṉakkūṟutal koḷḷattakkataṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟār. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vācippiṉāl māttiramaṉṟu; piṟa ārāyccikaḷālum mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭarēyeṉa uṟutippaṭu kiṉṟatu. ji.ār.haṇṭar, ār.ṭi.paṉērji, pantārkkar, rātā kumutā mukkarji, pōṉṟa ciṟanta ārāycci aṟiñar vaṭanāṭu, muḻumiyum tirāviṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum, cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ nākarikam, tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟum naṉku ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. aṉṉōr avvāṟu tuṇivataṟkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷa kāraṇaṅkaḷi ārāyntu maṟukkātum, tamatu kūṟṟukkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭātum vāḷā an nākarikam evaruṭiyateṉa viḷakkamākavilli eṉa oruvar kūṟuvārā ṉāl, akkūṟṟu avariṉ ārāyccik kuṟi pāṭṭit teḷḷitiṟ pulap paṭuttuvatākum. mokañcatarō arappā muttirikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷi ottavi varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiya camātikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avvaki eḻuttukkaḷ ilaṅkiyil kēkāli eṉṉum iṭattiluḷḷa maliyilum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mecapatēmiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa muttirikaḷilum avvaki eḻuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mokañca tarōvi aṭutta palucci°tāṉattil tirāviṭamoḻit toṭarpuṭiya pirākūymoḻi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. cumēriyarum, tirāviṭarum orē iṉa makkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar hāl kūṟiyuḷḷār. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ mokañ catarō eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiyavi eṉṟu pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār. 1hera° pātiriyār oli muṟiyāka vācitta mokañcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uccarippukkaḷ tamiḻc coṟkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi pōṉṟa pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl mokañcatarō nākarikam tamiḻaruṭiyatu eṉṟu naṉku teḷivākiṉṟatu. ivar mokañ catarō nākarikam toṭarpāka yātum aṟiyātiruppatu ivar kuṟiyē yākum.
irātā kumuta mukkarji eṉṉum pērāciriyar kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:- cintu veḷi nākarikam ivvāṟu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pēciya makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu peṟṟiruntateṉat tōṉṟum. tirāviṭamakkaḷ mattiyataric cāti eṉṉum kuḻuvic cērntavarkaḷāvār. in nākarikam vētakālattukku muṟpaṭṭa intiyanākarikamākum. piṟkāla intiya nākarikattil itaṉ cila pakutikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 1767ām āṇṭu variyil em.ī.tāma° (ā.nu. kūhṭiayaḷa) eṉpār, āriyar contamākat tamatu eḻuttukkaḷi uṇṭākkik koḷḷavilli eṉṟum, āṉāl avarkaḷ ataṉi intiya makkaḷiṭamiruntu aṟintu koṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. ikkaruttu nīṇṭakālam ārāycciyāḷarāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭavilli. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ, cemittiya eḻuttuk kaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟum, avi piṉīciya makkaḷāl ki.mu. 1000 variyil intiya nāṭṭiṟ parappappaṭṭateṉṟum karutappaṭṭaṉa. ikkaruttē nīṇṭakālam uṇmiyeṉak karutappaṭṭu vantatu. tāma° kūṟiyataṟku aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉ, pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ (Professor Langdon) eṉpār, pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyeṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. intu nākarikam - irātā kumuta mukarji1.
tirāviṭar mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷā?
tirāviṭa makkaḷ mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉṟu nīṇṭakālam ārāycciyāḷarāl karutappaṭalāyiṟṟu. cintuveḷip putiporuḷ ārāyccikkup piṉ hera° pātiriyār, ji.ār.haṇṭar, pērāciriyar, piṟāṅpōṭ, car.jāṉ.mārcal, cilṭi, ṭākṭar, hāl pōṉṟa ciṟanta ārāycci aṟiñarkaḷ, intiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu mēṟku āciyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷē mattiyatari makkaḷ āvar eṉat teḷivuṟak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattil carittira ārāyccip pulami naṭattum tiru. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷum ikkaruttiyē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ivvāṟu aṟiñar tuṇitaṟku ētuvāyuḷḷa kāraṇaṅkaḷi arāyntu pārātum, avarkaḷ kūṟṟukkaḷi maṟukkātum ārāycciyāḷar oruvar, intiyamakkaḷ mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷāvar eṉa vāvā kūṟuvarēl avar kūṟṟu eṭṭuṇiyum vali peṟātatākum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ārāycci yeṉṉum nūl eḻutiyavar, cintuveḷi makkaḷ mattiyatari nāṭukaḷi liruntu vantu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ nākarikam mattiya tari nākarikam eṉṟum kūṟiyavi ciṟitum valipeṟāmi kāṇka.
mattiyatari makkaḷ intiyarē
arappā mokañcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiyum nākarikattiyum naṉku ārāynta ṭākṭar ji.ār.haṇṭar kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:
1“āriyar varukikku muṉ tirāviṭar cintu veḷikaḷil vāḻnta varkaḷ eṉpataṟku ēṟṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷ pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. palucci°tāṉattil pirākūyar vaḻaṅkum moḻi, tirāviṭattukku iṉamāyiruppatāl ivvuṇmi valiyuṟukiṉṟatu. 2itu vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷilum pārkkac ciṟappuṭiyatu. hitarāpāttu, ceṉṉi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiḷaṟik kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa camātikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷil mokañca tarō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭavikaḷi otta eḻuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivviṭaṅkaḷ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷukkuriya nāṭukaḷākum. tirāviṭar cintuvil ki.mu. 1100-kku muṉ vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṭamiruntē cintu nākarikam avarkaḷ kikku māṟiṟṟu. “āriyar mattiya āciyāvilē kāṭṭu vāḻkkiyiṉarāyirunta kālattil intiya nāṭṭil vāḻnta tirāviṭar cemittiyari otta nākarikamuṭiyavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. cālamaṉ, haram eṉum aracar im makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu vittiruntatōṭu tirāviṭac coṟkaḷiyum tamatu moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉar. *cālamaṉ kālam ki.mu.1,000 variyil. ak kālattil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ cila epirēya moḻiyil vaḻaṅkiṉavāyiṉ, caṅkakālat tamiḻ akkālattamiḻiviṭa atika māṟutal aṭintirunta teṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. cālamaṉ kālattu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iṉṟiyat tamiḻilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mokañcatarō kālat tamiḻukkum caṅkakālat tamiḻukkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu, iṉṟiyat tamiḻuk kum caṅkakālat tamiḻukku muḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭi ottatākalām eṉak karututal piḻiyākātu. ivvuṇmiyic ciṟitum karuttiṟ koḷḷātu “tirāviṭa moḻikaḷippaṟṟiya ārāycci eḻutiyavar caṅkakālat tamiḻ iṉṟiya tamiḻilum pārkka vēṟāṉatu. hera° pātiriyār cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷukkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷip peytu vācippatu takātu; immuṟi moḻiyārāycci muṟiyāṉa vaḻikku māṟupaṭṭatu eṉak kuṟippiṭuvārāyiṉar. hera° pātiriyār kūṟṟukkaḷ eḷitil taḷḷiviṭat takkaṉavalla. avar tamiḻ aṟiyātavar. avar mokañcatarō eḻuttuk kaḷi olimuṟiyāka vācittapōtu avvolikaḷ kuṟippiṭum coṟkaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil iruppati avar piṟar vāyilākavē aṟintār. avar 1945il ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattil pala virivurikaḷ nikaḻttiṉār. appoḻutu avar mokañcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vācippukkaḷ. cilavaṟip pala paṭṭatārikaḷ, pērāciriyarkaḷ muṉṉili yil kūṟiṉār. avar kūṟṟukkaḷi nērilō eḻuttumūlamō evarum maṟukkavilli. muṉam uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉ aṭiyāḷamēyākum. mēṟ paṭi tirāviṭamoḻikaḷiṉ ārāycci eḻutiyavarum, hera° pātiri yāriṉ virivurikaḷik kēṭṭu makiḻntavarkaḷuḷ oruvarāvar. iṉṟum pampāyil paḻañcarittira ārāycci naṭattivarum hera° pātiriyāri itu toṭarpāka viṉāvi avar uṇmi aṟiyalāmaṉṟō?
tirukkuṟaḷ, vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppā?
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ pala pakutikaḷ cāṇakkiyar ceyta artta cāttirattiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭateṉac cilar kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. cāṇakkiyar kāñcipurattiṉiṉṟum vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu mayūra cantirakuptaṉiṉ mantiriyāyirunta tamiḻar. 1ivar vaṭamoḻiyil artta cāttiram mutaliya pala nūlkaḷ ceytu pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉār. accuvitti kaṇṭupiṭik kappaṭṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa paṭikaḷ veḷivarum ik kālattilēyēpala nūlkaḷ namakkuk kiṭikka villi. cāṇakkiyar ceytuḷḷa artta cāttira nūl teṉṉāṭṭut tiruvaḷḷuvarukkuk kiṭittatu eṉa nampamuṭiyātu. teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya cila ariya karuttukkaḷiyē cāṇakkiyar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceytu pukaḻ aṭintār. tiruvaḷḷuvarum teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya atē karuttiṉi muṟippaṭutti nūl ceytār. iruvarum tamiḻarātalālum, iruvarukkum nūl ceyvataṟku ātāram oṉṟu ātalālum, artta cāttiramum tirukkuṟaḷum pala iṭaṅkaḷil orē vakiyākak kāṇappaṭutal viyappu aṉṟeṉṟu. āriyarukku nūlkaḷic ceytu utaviya tamiḻarkaḷuḷ cāṇakkiyar1 oruvarāvar.
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ kālam
tirukkuṟaḷiṉ kālattiṉ kīḻ elli ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉpataṟku uriya ātāram uḷḷatu. cilappatikāram ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭatu. cilappatikārattil tirukkuṟaḷippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikārattukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.2
moḻinaṭi, collāṭci mutaliya cilavaṟṟi māttiram koṇṭu cilar tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷ, kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar kūṟṟukkaḷ vali peṟumāṟilli. kālam kuṟippataṟkuriya pala cāṉṟukaḷōṭu collāṭci mutaliyaṉavum oru cāṉṟākumēyaṉṟi, avikaḷikkoṇṭu māttiram nūlkaḷiṉ kālattit tuṇintuviṭutal kūṭātu. iṉṟu āṉanta vikaṭaṉil eḻutap paṭum naṭikkum karantit tamiḻccaṅkattāriṉ māta itaḻil eḻutappaṭum naṭikkum ettuṇi vēṟupāṭu uḷatu. ivvēṟupāṭṭik koṇṭu āṉanta vikaṭaṉum, tamiḻppoḻilum vevvēṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil uḷḷaṉa eṉak kūṟalāmā?
cilappatikārattiṉ piṟpakuti piṟarevarālō eḻutic cērkkap paṭṭateṉṉum ātāramaṟṟa koḷki oṉṟum cila ārāycciyāḷarāl kiḷappappaṭukiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattiṉ piṟpakuti piṟarāl ceyyappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku ātāramilli. atil kūṟappaṭṭaṉa cila uṇmiyallātaṉa pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa veṉil, avi ak kālatti aṭuttirunta meka°tīṉa° pōṉṟa varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷum, purāṇakārarkaḷum kūṟiya vikaḷiviṭa nampat takkaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉpati nōkki uṇmi teḷital vēṇṭum.
cilappatikāra kālam
cilappatikārattil,
“āṭittiṅkaṭ pēriruṭ pakkat
taḻalcēr kuṭṭat taṭṭami ñāṉṟu
veḷḷi vārat toḷḷeri yuṇṇa
vuricāṉ maturiyō ṭracukē ṭuṟumeṉu
muṟiyu muṇṭē”
eṉavarum cōtiṭak kuṟippiṉ kālam ki.mu.202 eṉa ṭākṭar kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkārum, ki.pi.171 eṉa1 mu.ḻu. cēṣa aiyarum muṟiyē kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. cēṣa aiyaravarkaḷ tamatu muṭivik kūṟumuṉ, ṭākṭar kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷatiyum ārāyntu pārttuḷḷār. 11-m paripāṭaliṟ kāṇappaṭum cōtiṭak kuṟippiṉ kālam ki.pi.196-jūṉµ 28² (28th June 196 A.C.) eṉa mu.ḻu.caṅkar eṉpār kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ ellōrum caṅkakālattiṉ kīḻ elli ki.pi, mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṉum muṭivukku vantuḷḷārkaḷ. cilappati kāramum maṇimēkaliyum caṅkakāla nūlkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikārañ ceytavar cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tampiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikku ālayam eṭuttu viḻāk koṇṭāṭiya kālattil pala nāṭṭu aracarkaḷ aṅkuc ceṉṟiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ ilaṅkik kayavākum oruvaṉ. ilaṅkik kayavāku ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil vāḻntavaṉ. ilaṅkik kayavākuviṉ kālam aṟiyappaṭutaliṉāl, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉiṉ kālamum, avaṉ kālattukku muṉṉum piṉṉum irunta cila aracarkaḷiṉ kālaṅkaḷum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē cilappatikāram, tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, aracarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ cilariṉ kālaṅkaḷi variyaṟuppataṟku ellik kalpōṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattil tirukkuṟaḷ eṭuttu āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikārat tukku muṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉat tuṇiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. karikālaṉ kāviriyiṉ āṇiyik kaṭṭiyatippaṟṟip paṭṭiṉappāli kūṟukiṉṟatu. muṉpu cōḻa aracaṉ, kāviri aṇi kaṭṭuvataṟkuc ciṟiyākap piṭittucceṉṟa ilaṅki makkaḷik kayavāku mīṭṭukkoṇṭu vantatōṭu, pattiṉiyiṉ cilampiyum koṇṭuvantāṉ eṉṟu ciṅkaḷa nūlākiya irācāvaḷi kūṟukiṉṟatu. kayavāku pattiṉi viḻāvukkuc cēra nāṭu ceṉṟiruntāṉ eṉac cilappatikāram kūṟukiṉṟatu. ilaṅki nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cāliyil ki.pi.āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil cetukkappaṭṭateṉṟu karutappaṭum kaṇṇakic cili oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṇṇaki vaḻipāṭu ilaṅki yil mikappaḻamiyē vērūṉṟiyuḷḷateṉa pērāciriyar āṉantak kumāracāmi avarkaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ilaṅki varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṉum nūlil itaṉi car.pi.aruṇācalam avarkaḷum ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār. ciṅkaḷavar viḻākkaḷil pattiṉic cilampu ūrvalattil eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatu. ciṅkaḷa makkaḷ putta ālayaṅkaḷil vittu vaḻipaṭṭa pattiṉic cilampukaḷ palavaṟṟi iṉṟum koḻumpu nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cāliyil kāṇalām. cilappatikārattip paṟṟit tamiḻppulavarkaḷum piṟarum naṉku aṟiya vantatu atu acciṭappaṭṭa piṉpēyākum. iṟṟikku nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ilaṅkiyil camarācaṉ eṉṉum pulavar oruvar kaṭalōṭṭukkāti eṉṉum nūl ceytār. annūl ituvariyil accēṟavivli appulavar tām collum kaṭalōṭṭukkāti cilappatikāratti ātāramākak koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭateṉak kūṟukiṉṟār. āṉāl atil colvaṉa cilappati kārattil kāṇappaṭavilli. itaṉāl iṟṟikku nāṉūṟu allatu ainnūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉum tamiḻppulavarkaḷ perumpālōr cilappatikārattip paṟṟi peyaraḷavil aṟintiruntārkaḷ. eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ciṅkaḷattil irācāvaḷi ceytavarkaḷ cilappatikārattip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭirukka māṭṭārkaḷ. cilappatikārattippaṟṟiyē aṟiyātār, kayavāku pattiṉic cilampik koṇṭu vantāṉeṉṟum, kayavākuvukku muṉ ciṟiyākap paṭikkappaṭṭa ciṅkaḷavar kāviri aṇi kaṭṭap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷavi, kayavāku pattiṉik kaṭavuḷiṉ viḻāvukku vantiruntāṉeṉṟum, avaṉ ilaṅki yil pattiṉikkuk kōyil amintāṉeṉṟum kūṟum varalāṟṟi ottuḷḷaṉa. ākavē iv voṟṟumikaḷi ātāramaṟṟaṉa eṉṟu taḷḷi viṭutal muṭiyātu.
S.K. cēṣa aiyaravarkaḷ caṅkakālac cēranāṭu eṉṉum nūlil cilappatikāra kālattip paṟṟi ārāyntu kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
paḻaṅkālap pulavarkaḷ tām niṉittapaṭi cōtiṭak kuṟippuk kaḷik kūṟiṉārkaḷ eṉa nām karututal tavaṟu. maturiyit tī uṇṭatākac cilappatikāram kūṟumkālam. ki.pi.171-m āṇṭu aṭiµ veḷḷivāram irupattāṟāvatu nāḷākum. ak kālattil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vāḻntāṉ. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattil kaṭal vāṇikam ucca nili aṭintiruntatu. itu piḷiṉi eṉpavar (ki.pi.161-maraṇam) muciṟiyip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷāl naṉkuviḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. intiyac carittirattil kālaṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭuvataṟkuc cilappatikāram ellik kallāka virukkiṉṟatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭu aracar mītu paṭi eṭuttāṉ eṉac collap paṭukiṉṟatu. avaṉ kaṅkik kariyil pōr ceytapōtu nūṟṟuvarkaṉṉar avaṉukku utaviyaḷittaṉar. avaṉi etirttavarkaḷuḷ, vicayar, pāla kumārar, uruttirar mutaliya peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷi veṉṟapiṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aṅkiruntu pattiṉic cili ceyvataṟku oru kalli eṭuttuvantāṉ. kalnāṭṭiyapōtu pala nāṭṭu aracar vantirun tārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ ilaṅkik kayavāku aracaṉ oruvaṉāvaṉ. ceṅ kuṭṭuvaṉallāta aracar kālattil vaṭanāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭi eṭukka iyalātavāṟu teṉṉintiya aracar palam kuṉṟiyiruntaṉar. vaṭintiya varalāṟṟi nōkkumpōtu, iraṇṭumūṉṟu muṟi māttiram teṉ ṉintiyar vaṭakku nōkkip paṭi eṭukkat takkatāyintatu. ki.mu.mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil cantirakuptaṉ pimpicāraṉ acōkaṉ mutaliyōr kālattil teṉṉāṭṭār vaṭakkē paṭi eṭuttirukkamuṭiyātu. puṣya mittiraṉ kālattilum itu nikaḻntirukkamuṭiyātu. puṣya mittiraṉ ki.mu.234-kkum ki.mu.184-kku miṭiyil āṭci purintāṉ. cuṅka aracariṉ piṟkāla āṭci kuḻappamaṭintiruntatu cātavākaṉar allatu cartakarīkayar atikāram eytamuyaṉṟu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil avarkaḷ kaṇṇuvariṉ āṭciyik kippaṟṟiṉārkaḷ. nācik paṭṭiyattil srīcātakarṇiyiṉ peyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. puṣyamittira ṉiṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ cuṅkarum kaṇṇuvarum kuḻappam aṭintirunta kālattil oru paṭi eṭuppukku iṭam iruntatu. itu ki.mu.148-kkum ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭiyil. kutami puttira srīcātakarṇikkuppiṉ (ki.pi.109-135) pulamāy eṉpavaṉ 35 āṇṭukaḷ āṭcipurintāṉ. avaṉ, mutalām uruttiratāmaṉōṭu mōtik koṇṭāṉ. uruttiratāmaṉ kutami puttira srīcātakarṇi cākaratariṭamiruntu veṉṟa nāṭukaḷip piṭuṅkikkoṇṭāṉ. āṉāl kutami puttiraṉ yāññasrī (ki.pi.173-202) pulamāy iḻanta cila nāṭukaḷi mīṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ, cātavākaṉar akkālattil teṉṉāṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppi etirkkak kūṭātavāṟu palavīṉamaṭintiruntārkaḷ. yāñña srīyiṉ kālattukkuppiṉ iruṭ kālam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. piṉpu mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭi aṭikiṉṟōm. nāṉkām aintām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākiya kuptarkaḷ kālattippaṟṟi naṉṟākat terikiṉṟatu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭu vākaṭariṉ kālamākum. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil atikāram paṭitta pallava cāḷukkiya kālattukku varukiṉ ṟōm. cāḷukkiyar ki.pi.753il irāṣṭira kuptarāl vīḻttappaṭṭārkaḷ. nālām nuṟṟāṇṭu mutal aṭutta nāṉūṟu āṇṭu variyil vaṭanāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭi eṭuppataṟku ēṟṟa kālamāka irukkavilli.
maturi ki.pi.171il tīppaṭṭatāyiṉum, appōtu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ āṭcipurintukoṇṭiruntāṉāyiṉum, kaṇṇaki vāṉulakaṭintapiṉ, nūṟṟuvarkaṉṉar utavi koṭuppatāka uṟuti kūṟiyapiṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṉmītu paṭi eṭuttāṉākavēṇṭum, nūṟṟuvarkaṉṉar eṉpōr cātakarṇikaḷē. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉatu nāṭṭiviṭṭu 32 tiṅkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟiruntāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉ kaṅkik kariyiliruntāṉ. avaṉ kaṅkikkariyilirunta kālam ki.pi.175-ṉ muṟpakuti eṉak kūṟalām. tamatu muṉṉōṉāṉa pulumāy iḻanta nāṭukaḷic cātakarṇikaḷ peṟuvataṟkuc cātakarṇikaḷ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ utaviyi vēṇṭiyirukkalām. itaṉāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ yāññasrīyiṉ kālattavaṉ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. itaṉāl nām ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ vaṭanāṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppippaṟṟi naṉku aṟiyakkūṭiyatāyirukkiṉṟatu. cātakarṇikaḷ tamatu nōkkam niṟivēṟiyapōtu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ viruppiyum niṟi vēṟṟiṉārkaḷ. pala āriya aracar ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉi etirttārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷil oruvaṉ pālkumāraṉiṉ makaṉ vicayaṉ eṉavum paṭukiṉṟāṉ. pālakumāraṉ eṉpavaṉ tālimi kūṟum pāliyō kurū°. (Baleokouros,) cila varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ pāliyōkurū° cātavākaṉa aracaruḷ oruvaṉeṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṉuṭiya makaṉē ceṅ kuṭṭuvaṉik kaṅkikkariyil etirttavaṉākalām. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉāl tōṟkaṭikkappaṭṭa iṉṉoruvaṉ uruttiraṉ. ivaṉ uruttiracaṉmaṉ allatu uruttiracēṉaṉākalām. ivaṉiṭatti liruntē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cātavākaṉar iḻnta nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. kaṅkip pōr eṉpatu yāññasrīyālum avaṉ tuṇivarkaḷālum vaṭanāṭṭu aracarukku etirāka naṭanta pōrākum.
kaṇṇakikkuk kalnāṭṭiyapōtu vantirunta aracaruḷ ilaṅkik kayavāku oruvaṉ eṉac cilappatikāram kūṟukiṉṟatu. makāvamicattiṉpaṭi kayavākuviṉ āṭcik kālam ki.pi.173-mutal 191vari. irācāvaḷi eṉṉum ciṅkaḷa nūl, kayavāku pattiṉik kaṭavuḷiṉ cilampi eṭuttuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. kayavāku pattiṉik kaṭavuḷukkuk kōyil eṭuttāṉ eṉṉum varalāṟṟōṭu itu ottirukkiṉṟatu. pattiṉikkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ilaṅkiyil paḻaṅkāla mutal uḷḷatu eṉṟu āṉantak kumāracāmi avarkaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār - (J.R.A.S.1909 p22).
maturi ki.pi.171-l tīppaṭṭatu eṉṉum koḷki palavakiyāl uṟutippaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ kālam iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉak kūṟuvatu poruttamāyirukkiṉṟatu.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum kayavākuvum orēkālattavarkaḷ eṉṉum koḷki eḷitil taḷḷiviṭakkūṭiyataṉṟu. cilappatikāram makāvamicam mutaliya nūlkaḷ varalāṟṟut toṭarpāṉavi.

nīti neṟi viḷakkam
mūlamum uriyum
āṅkila moḻi peyarppum
muṉṉuri
piṟkāla nūlkaḷuḷ nītineṟi viḷakkam col naṭi poruṭ ceṟivu mutaliyavikaḷāl ciṟappamintatu. ak kālattil maturiyi āṇṭa tirumali nāyakaṉ (1623-1659) eṉṉum aracaṉiṉ vēṇṭu kōḷukku icintu kumarakuruparar nītineṟi viḷakkam eṉṉum nūlit tirukkuṟaḷiṉ cāramākac ceytāreṉpatu parampari varalāṟu. mēli nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum kiṟittuva matattip parappum poruṭṭu intiya nāṭu pōntār tamatu koḷkikaḷip parappum poruṭṭu makkaḷ pēcum moḻikaḷip payilum kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyiruntatu. tamiḻip payilat toṭaṅkiya mēl nāṭṭavar tamiḻil uḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum oppuyar villāta nūlik kaṇṭu viyappaṭintu ataṉik kaṟṟu ataṉit tattam moḻikaḷil moḻi peyarttukkoṇṭaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷi oppa mēlnāṭṭavariṉ kavaṉatti īrtta iṉṉoru nūl nītineṟi viḷakkam. in nūli sṭōks (H. Stokes, I.C.S) 1830il accēṟṟi veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷār. vēṟu palar itaṉi āṅkilattil moḻi peyarttuḷḷārkaḷ. ip patippu putiya teḷḷiya uriyōṭum āṅkila moḻipeyarppōṭum kūṭiyatu.
kumarakuruparar
nītineṟi viḷakkam eṉṉum ariya nīti nūli aruḷic ceyta kumarakurupara cuvāmikaḷ 1675ām āṇṭu (ciṟī vikuntattiṟ piṟantār. piṟantu aintu āṇṭaḷavum ivar ūmi yāyiruntār. piṉpu ivar pēcum vaṉmi aṭintār. ivar teṉṉintiyāviṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷukkut tala yāttiri ceyta piṉ 1658il kācikkuc ceṉṟār. aṅku ivar mukamatiya aracaṉāl naṉku matikkappaṭṭu avaṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭa nilattil maṭam oṉṟik kaṭṭiṉār. 1988il ivar kālamāṉār. ivaruṭiya pāṭalkaḷ coṟcuvi poruṭcuvi nirampiyavi. ivar ceyta nūlkaḷ 1. kantar kaliveṇpā, 2. mīṉāṭci piḷḷit tamiḻ, 3. mīṉāṭci kuṟam, 4. mīṉāṭci iraṭṭi maṇimāli, 5. paṇṭāra mummaṇik kōvi, 6. muttukkumāra cuvāmi piḷḷit tamiḻ, 7. citampara mummaṇik kōvi, 8. nītineṟi viḷakkam, 9. maturik kalampakam, 10. kācik kalampakam, 11. citamparac ceyyuṭ kōvi eṉpaṉavām.
ceṉṉi
1.4.1949 na.ci. kantiyā
nīti neṟi viḷakkam
mūlamum uriyum
āṅkila moḻipeyarppum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu
nīriṟ kumiḻi iḷami niṟicelvam
nīriṟ curuṭṭu neṭuntirikaḷ - nīril
eḻuttākum yākki namaraṅkāḷ eṉṉē
vaḻuttāta tempirāṉ maṉṟu. 1
1. Youth is a bubble on the water: riches in full abundance are the long waves that roll on the water; the body is a writing on the water. Why, O my friends, worship we not the Court of our God?
(patavuri) iḷami nīril kumiḻi - iḷamip paruvam nīril kumiḻi pōṉṟu nili illātatu; niṟi celvam nīril curuṭṭu neṭum tirikaḷ - niṟinta celvam (kaṭal) nīrilē curuṇṭu varukiṉṟa nīṇṭa alikaḷ pōṉṟu tōṉṟi maṟivatu; yākki nīril eḻuttākum - uṭampu taṇṇīril eḻutiya eḻuttup pōl virivil maṟintu pōvatu; (ātaliṉ) namaraṅkāḷ - nammavarē! empirāṉ maṉṟu vaḻuttātatu eṉ - ematu kaṭavuḷiṉ capiyit tutiyātatu ēṉō?
(karutturi) uṭampu niliyaṟṟatu. ātalāl atu nilit tirukkum pōtē kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum.
kalviyē uṟutiyāṉa tuṇi
aṟamporu ḷiṉpamum vīṭum payakkum
puṟaṅkaṭi nalliciyum nāṭṭum - uṟuṅkavaloṉ
ṟuṟṟuḻiyuṅ kikoṭukkuṅ kalviyi ṉūṅkilli
ciṟṟuyirk kuṟṟa tuṇi. 2
2. It will bestow virtue, riches, pleasure and Heaven. It will establish a good report in the world, and when any great affliction may betide, it will afford a hand; short-lived mortals have no surer stay than learning.
(pa-u) (kalvi) aṟamporuḷ iṉpamum vīṭum payakkum - (kalvi) aṟattiyum poruḷiyum iṉpattiyum vīṭu pēṟṟiyum koṭukkum; puṟam kaṭi nal iciyum nāṭṭum - ulakil nalla pukaḻiyum nili niṟuttum; uṟum kaval oṉṟu uṟṟuḻiyum ki koṭukkum - periya tuṉpam oṉṟu nērntaviṭattum tuṇi ceyyum; (ātaliṉ) ciṟu uyirkku uṟṟa tuṇi kalviyiṉ ūṅku illi - ākavē ciṟiya vāḻnāḷuṭiya makkaḷukku uṟutiyākiya tuṇi kalviyilum mēlāka vēṟoṉṟilli.
puṟaṅkaṭi - puṟattē.
(ka-u) kalvi makkaḷukku ellāp payaṉum koṭuttu utavattakkatu. ātalāl ataṉik kaṟṟal vēṇṭum.
kalvi kaṟpatu tuṉpamāyiṉum atu piṉ iṉpantarum
toṭaṅkuṅkāl tuṉpamāy iṉpam payakkum
maṭaṅkoṉ ṟaṟivakaṟṟuṅ kalvi - neṭuṅkāmam
muṟpayakkuñ ciṉṉīra viṉpattiṉ muṟṟiḻāy
piṟpayakkum pīḻi peritu. 3
3. O thou adoned with perfect jewels! Learning at first pinful, will afterwards afford pleasure. It will destroy ignorance and extend knowledge. But the pin, which succeeds to the short-lived pleasure of immoderate lust,is great. 
(pa-u) muṟṟu iḻāy - muṟṟāka muṭinta vēlippāṭukaḷuṭiya nakikaḷi aṇintavaṉē; maṭam koṉṟu aṟivu akaṟṟum kalvi - aṟiyāmiyik keṭuttu aṟivi vicālappaṭuttum kalvi; toṭaṅkuṅ kāl tuṉpamāy iṉpam payakkum - kaṟkat toṭaṅkum pōtu tuṉpam taruvatākip piṉpu makiḻcciyik koṭukkum; neṭum kāmam - mikunta kāmam; muṟpayakkum ciṉnīra iṉpattil piṟpayakkum piḻi peritu - mutalil koṭukkum ciṟitu nēra iṉpattilum pārkkap piṉpu koṭukkum tuṉpam peritākum.
(ka-u) kalvi toṭakkattil tuṉpamākat tōṉṟippiṉ iṉpan tarum; kāmam mutalil iṉpamākat tōṉṟippiṉ tuṉpantarum.
capiyi alaṅkarikkum kalvi
kalviyē kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirap peṇṭirkkuc
celvap putalvaṉē tīṅkaviyāc - colvaḷam
mallal veṟukkiyā māṇavi maṇṇuṟuttuñ
celvamu muṇṭu cilarkku. 4
4. Blest with learning as a chaste spouse, with sweet poctry as the darling son of that spouse, and with fluency of language as a store of riches, there are some whose wealth adorns the illustrious assembly of the learned.
(pa-u) cilarkku kalviyē kaṟpuṭip peṇṭir (ā) - kalvi kaṟkum cilarkkuk kalviyē kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyākavum; appeṇṭirkkut tīm kaviyē celvap putalvaṉ ā - am maṉivikku iṉiya pāṭalē celvap putalvaṉākavum; colvaḷam mallal veṟukki ā - collum tiṟamiyē niṟinta celvamākavum; māṇ avi maṇṇuṟuttum celvamum uṇṭu - periya aṟivuṭiyōr capiyi alaṅkarikkiṉṟa celvamum uṇṭu.
(ka-u) cilarukku māttiram nuliyaṟṟumāṟṟalōṭu ciṟanta pēcumāṟṟalum uṇṭu.
kalvikkuc ciṟappu colvaṉmi
ettuṇiya vāyiṉuṅ kalvi iṭamaṟin
tuyttuṇar villeṉiṉ illākum - uyttuṇarntum
colvaṉmi yiṉṟeṉiṉ eṉṉākum aḵtuṇṭēl
poṉmalar nāṟṟa muṭittu. 5
5. Learning, however extensive, will be useless, unless there be discretion to display it in the proper place; and even then, without the power of language, of what avil is it? With that, it is a flower of gold that possesses fragrance.
(pa-u) kalvi ettuṇiya āyiṉum - (oruvaṉukkuk) kalvi evvaḷavu irunta pōtum; iṭam aṟintu uyttu uṇarvu il eṉil illākum - nūliṉ iṭatti aṟintu ārāyntu aṟiyum aṟivu illiyāyiṉ atu payaṉ paṭātākum; uyttuṇarntum - avvāṟu ārāyntu aṟintum; colvaṉmi iṉṟu eṉiṉ eṉṉākum - pēcum āṟṟal illiyāyiṉ eṉṉa payaṉuṇṭākum? aḵtu uṇṭēl poṉ malar nāṟṟam uṭittu - pēccuvaṉmi iruntāl poṉ malar maṇam peṟṟatu pōṉṟatākum.
(ka-u) iṭamaṟintu poruḷaṟitalum pēccu vaṉmiyum vēṇṭum.
avi añcuvār kalvi ciṟantataṉṟu
aviyañci meyvitirppār kalviyum kallār
aviyañcā ākulac collum - naviyañci
īttuṇṇār celvamum nalkūrntā riṉṉalamum
pūttaliṟ pūvāmi naṉṟu. 6
6. The learning of those whose frame trembles with diffidence before the assembly - the frivolous loquacity of the ignorant who feel no awe in the assembly - the wealth of those who do not conscientiously bestow alms before they eat - and the merit of a poor man, are things, the absence of which is better than their existance.
(pa-u) avi añci mey vitirppār kalviyum - capikkup payantu uṭampu naṭuṅkukiṉṟavariṉ kalviyum; kallār - paṭiyātavariṉ; avi añcā ākulac collum - capikkup payappaṭāta āravārac collum; navi añci īttu uṇṇār celvamum - kuṟṟattukku añci (vaṟiya varkkuk) koṭuttuṇṇātavariṉ celvamum; nalkūrntār iṉ nalamum - vaṟiyavariṉ iṉiya kuṇaṅkaḷum; pūttalil pūvāmi naṉṟu - tōṉṟutalil tōṉṟāmi nallatu.
(ka-u) capikku añcum kalviyaṟivāl payaṉilli.
ūṉuṭampilum pukaḻuṭampē mēlāṉatu
kalimakaḷ vāḻkki mukatta teṉiṉum
malaravaṉ vaṇṭamiḻōrk kovvāṉ - malaravaṉcey
veṟṟuṭampu māyvaṉapōl māyā pukaḻkoṇṭu
maṟṟivar ceyyum uṭampu. 7
7. Though Saraswati dwell in the face of both, Brahma may not vie with the illustrious Tamil bards; for the bodies, which these form by their prises, will not perish like the empty bodies of Brahma’s creation.
(pa-u) kalimakaḷ vāḻkki mukattatu eṉiṉum - nāmakaḷiṉ vāḻkki (piramaṉatu) mukattilum (kaṟṟavar) mukattilumāṉālum; malaravaṉ vaṇ tamiḻōrkku ovvāṉ - piramā pukaḻuṭiya tamiḻp pulavarukku oppāka māṭṭāṉ; (ēṉeṉṟāl) malaravaṉ cey veṟṟu uṭampu māyvaṉa pōl - piramā ceykiṉṟa veṟiya (pukaḻillāta) uṭampu aḻivatip pōla; ivar pukaḻkoṇṭu ceyyum maṟṟu uṭampu māyā - ippulavar pukaḻik koṇṭu ceykiṉṟa vēṟu vakiyāṉa uṭampu aḻiya māṭṭātu.
(ka-u) kalviyāluṇṭākum pukaḻ eṉṟum aḻiyātu.
capiyil utavāk kalvi
neṭumpakaltām kaṟṟa aviyat tutavā
tuṭintuḷā ruṭkuvaruṅ kalvi - kaṭumpakal
ētilāṉ pāṟkaṇṭa illiṉum pollātē
tīteṉṟu nīppari tāl. 8
8. The shrinking learning of men broken by long study, which is not avilable in public, is worse than a wife seen in broad day-light in a neighbour’s, company, for it is an evil that cannot be put away.
(pa-u) neṭum pakal tām kaṟṟa - nīṇṭakālam tām paṭittavikaḷi; aviyattu utavātu - capiyil eṭutturikka muṭiyāmal; uṭintu uḷār - maṉam varunti irukkiṉṟavarkaḷatu; uṭkuvarum kalvi - accattik kāṭṭum kalvi; kaṭum pakal-naṭuppakalil; ētilāṉpāl kaṇṭa illiṉum pollātu - ayalavaṉiṭattil kaṇṭa oruvaṉuṭiya maṉiviyilum pārkkat tīyatākum; (ēṉeṉil) tītu eṉṟu nīppu aritu āl - (itu) tīyateṉṟu vilakki viṭutaṟku muṭiyātiruppatāl.
(ka-u) capiyiṭattu utavāta kalviyāṟ payaṉilli.
paṭittavaṟṟi maṟantu putiyavaṟṟik kaṟṟal
varuntittāṅ kaṟṟaṉa vōmpātu maṟṟum
parintucila kaṟpāṉ toṭaṅkal - karuntaṉam
kittalatta uyttuc corintiṭ ṭaripparittāṅ
keyttup poruḷcey tiṭal. 9
9. The attempt of those who, without preserving that learning which they have acquired with difficulty, try to make fresh acquirements, is like throwing away gold from their hands and toiling to make a fortune by sifting dust in search of treasure.
(pa-u) varunti tām kaṟṟaṉa ōmpātu - muyaṉṟu tām paṭittavikaḷi (niṉivil vittup) pātukāvāmal; maṟṟum cila parintu kaṟpāṉ toṭaṅkal - vēṟu cilavaṟṟi varuntik kaṟkat toṭaṅkutal; ki talatta karuntaṉam uyttu corintiṭṭu - (oruvaṉ) kiyiṭattuḷḷa poṉṉi (nilattil) vīci eṟintuviṭṭu; āṅku - avviṭattilē; arippu arittu eyttup poruḷ ceytiṭal - aripparittu varuntip poruḷic cērppatu pōlākum.
(ka-u) paṭittavaṟṟi maṟantu putiyavaṟṟik kaṟkat toṭaṅkutal aṟiyāmi.
vaṟiyār kalvi viḷakkamaṭiyātu
eṉittuṇiya vēṉum ilampaṭṭār kalvi
tiṉittuṇiyuñ cīrpā ṭilavām - maṉittakkāḷ
māṇpila ḷāyiṉ maṇamakaṉ nallaṟam
pūṇṭa pulappaṭā pōl. 10
10. However great may be the learning of poor men, it will never be appreciated so much as a grin to tinay even as, if the mistress of the house possess not the qualifications which become a wife, the virtues, with which her husband is endued, will not become conspicuous.
(pa-u) maṉittakkāḷ māṇpu ilaḷ āyiṉ - maṉivi ciṟappillātavaḷāyiṉ; maṇamakaṉ pūṇṭa nal aṟam pulappaṭā. kaṇavaṉ koṇṭa illaṟam viḷakkamaṭiya māṭṭātu; pōl - atu pōla; ilam paṭṭār kalvi eṉittuṇiya vēṉum - vaṟiyavaruṭiya kalvi evvaḷavu peritāyiruntapōtum; tiṉi tuṇiyum cīrpāṭu ilavām - tiṉiyaḷavum ciṟappu aṭital illiyākum.
(ka-u) vaṟiyavar kalvi pirakācippatilli.
vaṟiyavar nallavarāyiṉum matippaṭiyār
iṉcollaṉ tāḻnaṭiya ṉuyiṉumoṉ ṟillāṉēl
vaṉcolli ṉallatu vāytiṟavā - eṉcoliṉum
kittuṭiyāṉ kāṟkīḻ otuṅkuṅ kaṭaṉñālam
pittuṭiya valla piṟa. 11
11. Though he may be courteous in speech, and humble in his behaviour, they will not open their lips to a poor man except in harsh words. Whatever a rich man may say they will crouch beneath his feet. Surely this world surrounded by the sea is full of madness.
(pa-u) iṉ collaṉ - (oruvaṉ) iṉiyacoṟ pēcukiṉṟavaṉum; tāḻnaṭiyaṉ āyiṉum - aṭakkamāṉa oḻukkamuṭiyavaṉumāyiṉum; oṉ ṟillāṉēl - avaṉ ciṟitu poruḷāvatu illāmal iruppāṉāṉāl; vaṉcolliṉ allatu - (ulakattavar avaṉiṭattu) kaṭiya coṟkaḷip pēcutalallatu; vāy tiṟavā - (iṉiya coṟkaḷip) pēcamāṭṭār; kittu uṭiyāṉ eṉ coliṉum - kiyiṟ poruḷuṭiyavaṉ etic coṉṉālum; kāl kīḻ otuṅkum - avaṉuṭiya kāliṉ kīḻ aṭaṅkuvar; kaṭal ñālam pittuṭiyatu - kaṭalcūḻnta ivvulakiluḷḷavarkaḷ pittiyam koṇṭavarāvar; piṟa alla - vēṟoṉṟum uṭiyavarallar.
(ka-u) vaṟiyavar nallavarāyiṉum matippeytār; celvar tīyavarāyiṉum matippaṭivar.
celvarip palar cūḻntu kuṟiyirappar
ivaṟaṉmi kaṇṭum uṭiyāri yārum
kuṟiyirantum kuṟṟēval ceypa - perituntām
muṟpakal nōlātār nōṟṟārip piṉcellal
kaṟpaṉṟē kallāmi yaṉṟu. 12
12. Although they see their covetousness, all urge their wants on the rich, and perform their mean behests. The submission of those who were not eminently devout in a former birth to those who were, is doubtless not from ignorance, but the decree of destiny.
(pa-u) ivaṟaṉmi kaṇṭum - īyāta taṉmiyi aṟintiruntum; yārum uṭiyāri kuṟi irantum kuṟṟēval ceypa - ellārum poruḷuṭiyavariṭam (ceṉṟu) tamatu kuṟiyi nīkkumpaṭi kēṭṭu (avar) paṇiviṭikaḷiyum ceyvar; muṉ pakal tām peritum nōlātār - muṟpiṟappil tām peritum tavam ceyyātavar; nōṟṟāri piṉ cellal kaṟpu aṉṟē - tavam ceytavarip piṉ ceṉṟu vēṇṭutal aṟivākum; kallāmi aṉṟu - (aḵtu) aṟiyāmi aṉṟu; ē - tēṟṟam.
(ka-u) poruḷ koṭāta ulōpiyarāyirunta pōtilum celvarip palar cūḻntu kuṟikaḷic colli paṇiviṭi ceyvar.
kaṟṟārkkuk kalviyē aḻaku
kaṟṟōrkkuk kalvi nalaṉē kalaṉallāl
maṟṟōraṇikalam vēṇṭāvām - muṟṟa
muḻumaṇip pūṇukkup pūṇvēṇṭā yārē
aḻakuk kaḻaku ceyvār 13
13. The learned need no other ornament than the excellence of learning. Nothing is wanting to adornan ornament set perfectly with every precious stone. Who would beautify beauty itself?
(pa-u) muṟṟa muḻumaṇi pūṇukku pūṇvēṇṭā - (vēlippāṭu) muṭivuṟumpaṭi muḻu māṇikkaṅkaḷ patitta āparaṇattukku aḻaku ceyvataṟku vēṟu āparaṇam vēṇṭiyatilli; (atupōla) kaṟṟōrkku kalvi nalaṉē kalaṉ allāl - paṭittavarkaḷukkup paṭippiṉ aḻakē aḻakāvatallatu; maṟṟu ōr aṇikalam vēṇṭāvām - (avarukku) vēṟu āparaṇam vēṇṭiyatilli; yārē aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvār - ēṉeṉil evar tām aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvar? (ceyyār eṉṟavāṟu)
(ka-u) kaṟṟavarukkuc ciṟanta aḻakāvatu kalviyē; piṟar matippeytuvataṟkuk kalviyillātavarip pōla avar tammi alaṅkarittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatilli.
ellāmaṟintavar ulakil illi
muṟṟu muṇarntava rilli muḻuvatūuṅ
kaṟṟaṉa meṉṟu kaḷiyaṟka - ciṟṟuḷiyāṟ
kallun takarun takarā kaṉaṅkuḻāy
kollulik kūṭattiṉāl. 14
14. There are none who understand every thing. Exult not in the idea that your learning is universal. The rock will give way to the small chisel of the mason; it will not, O thou adorned with massive ornaments, yield to the blacksmith’s hammer.
(pa-u) muṟṟum uṇarntavar illi - ellām aṟintavar (ivvulakattil) illi; mutuvatūum kaṟṟaṉam eṉṟu kaḷiyaṟka - (ākavē) ellāvaṟṟiyum paṭittaṟintu viṭṭōmeṉṟu cerukkuk koḷḷa vēṇṭām; kaṉam kuḻāy - pāramāṉa kātaṇiyi aṇintavaḷē; ciṟu uḷiyāl kallum takarum - ciṟiya uḷiyiṉāl pāṟikaḷum uṭiyum; kol ulik kūṭattiṉāl takarā - (āṉāl) kollaṉatu uliyiṭat tuḷḷa cammaṭṭiyiṉāl appāṟi uṭiyātu.
(ka-u) ulakil ellām aṟintavar illi; periya kalvi āḷar aṟiyāta karuttukkaḷ cila potumakkaḷiṭattum kāṇappaṭum; ākavē kalvic cerukkuk kūṭātu.
kaṟṟāri nōkkic ceruk koḻika
tammiṉ meliyāri nōkkit tamatuṭimi
ammā periteṉ ṟakamakiḻka - tammiṉum
kaṟṟāri nōkkik karuttaḻika kaṟṟatellām
eṟṟē ivarkku nām eṉṟu. 15
15. Contemplate those who are poorer than yourselves, and rejoice in the greatness of your possessions. Contemplate those who are more learned than yourselves, and destroy self-conceit, excliming what is all our learning to these!
(pa-u) tammiṉ meliyāri nōkki - tammik kāṭṭilum vaṟiyavarkaḷip pārttu; tamatu uṭimi ammā peritu eṉṟu akam makiḻka - ammā! tammiṭattiluḷḷatu periyatu eṉṟu maṉam makiḻtal vēṇṭum; tammiṉum kaṟṟāri nōkki - tammilum pārkkap paṭittavarip pārttu; nām kaṟṟatu ellām ivarkku eṟṟē eṉṟu karuttu aḻika - nām paṭittatellām ivar paṭippukku evvaḷaveṉṟu (cerukkāṉa) eṇṇatti viṭṭu viṭutal vēṇṭum.
(ka-u) tammilum vaṟiyavarik kāṇum pōtu tamatu celvam peritu eṉṟu makiḻa vēṇṭum; tammilum kaṟṟārik kāṇum pōtu tamatu kalvi aṟpam eṉṟu cerukku oḻiya vēṇṭum.
celvattuk kaḻaku paṇivu
kalvi yuṭimi poruḷuṭimi yeṉṟiraṇṭu
celvamuñ celva meṉappaṭum - illār
kuṟiyirantu tammuṉṉar niṟpapōṟ ṟāmun
talivaṇaṅkit tāḻap peṟiṉ. 16
16. The wealth, which consists in the possession of learning, and the possession of riches. may be termed wealth indeed, if its possessors bow the head with humility, as the destitute who stand begging before them.
(pa-u) illār-vaṟiyavar; kuṟi irantu tam muṉṉar niṟpapōl - tamatu kuṟiyi vēṇṭit tamakku muṉṉāl niṟpatu pōla; tāmum tali vaṇaṅkit tāḻap peṟiṉ - tāṅkaḷum taliyāl vaṇaṅkit tāḻntu niṟpārkaḷāyiṉ; kalvi uṭimi poruḷ uṭimi eṉṟu iraṇṭu celvamum - kalvic celvam poruṭcelvam eṉṉum iraṇṭu celvaṅkaḷum; celvam eṉappaṭum - ciṟanta celvaṅkaḷ eṉṟu (aṟivuṭiyōrāl) collappaṭum.
(ka-u) kalvic celvattukkum poruṭ celvattukkum ciṟapput taruvatu paṇivē.
aṟivillātārukku aṟiñar tāḻntu naṭappar
ākkam periyār ciṟiyā riṭippaṭṭa
mīccelavu kāṇiṉ naṉitāḻpa - tūkkiṉ
meliyatu mēṉmē leḻaccellac cella
valitaṉṟē tāḻun tuli. 17
17. If men of real dignity perceive presumption in the conduct of their inferiors, they will behave with more humility. In weighing, while the lighter rises higher and higher, does not the heavier sink down the scale?
(pa-u) tūkkiṉ - niṟuttup pārkkumpōtu; tuli meliyatu mēṉmēl eḻa cellac cella - tarācil pāraṅ kuṟinta poruḷuḷḷa taṭṭu mēlum mēlum uyarac cellac cella; valitu aṉṟē tāḻum - pāramāṉa poruḷuḷḷataṉṟō tāḻum; (atu pōla) ākkam periyār - aṟivālum celvattālum periyavar; ciṟiyōr iṭipaṭṭa mī celavu kāṇiṉ naṉitāḻpa - (avviraṇṭumillāta) ciṟiyavariṭattu varampukaṭanta naṭatti kaṇṭāl avarkaḷukku mikavum tāḻntu naṭappar.
(ka-u) aṟivillātavar varampu kaṭantu naṭappiṉ aṟivuṭiyār paṇivar.
taṟpukaḻcci tītu
vilakkiya ōmpi vitittaṉavē ceyyum
nalattakiyār nalviṉiyun tītē - pulap pakiyi
veṉṟaṉam nalloḻukkil niṉṟēm piṟaveṉṟu
tampāṭu tammiṟ koḷiṉ. 18
18. Even the good works of moral men, who shun what is to be avoided, and practise what has been enjoined, become evil, if, proudly boasting that they have conquered the enmity of the senses, and stood stead fastly in a virtuous course, they themselves dwell on their own merits.
(pa-u) pulam pakiyi piṟa veṉṟaṉam - aimpulaṉkaḷākiya pakiyiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum veṉṟuviṭṭōm; nal oḻukkil niṉṟēm eṉṟu - nalla oḻukka neṟiyil niṟkiṉṟōmeṉṟu; tam pāṭu tammil koḷiṉ - tammuṭiya perumiyit tāmē niṉittuk koḷvā rāyiṉ; vilakkiya ōmpi - vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟic ceyyāmal kāttu; vitittaṉavē ceyyum nalattakiyār nalviṉiyum tītē - (ceyyattakkaṉa veṉṟu) vitikkap paṭṭavikaḷic ceyyum nalla taṉmiyuṭiyavarkaḷ ceyyum nalla ceyalkaḷum tīyaṉavākum.
(ka-u) uyarnta oḻukka neṟiyil niṟpavarkaḷum tammip pukaḻtal tīyatākum.
taṉṉip pukaḻāmiyē naṉṟu
taṉṉi viyappippāṉ taṟpukaḻtal tīccuṭar
naṉṉīr corintu vaḷarttaṟṟāl - taṉṉi
viyavāmi yaṉṟē viyappāva tiṉpam
nayavāmi yaṉṟē nalam. 19
19. To prise oneself in order to attract admiration, is like feeding the flame with pure water - Is not the absence of self-admiration that which is to be admired? Is not happiness freedom from the desire of pleasure?
(pa-u) taṉṉi viyappippāṉ taṉ pukaḻtal - (oruvaṉ) taṉṉip piṟar matikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutit taṉṉip pukaḻtal; nal nīr corintu tī cuṭar vaḷarttaṟṟāl - nalla nīri ūṟṟi neruppuc cuṭari vaḷarttatu pōlākum; taṉṉi viyavāmi aṉṟē viyappu āvatu - taṉṉip pukaḻātu iruppataṉṟō nalla matippāvatu; iṉpam nayavāmi aṉṟē nalam - iṉpatti virumpāmal iruppatallavō iṉpamāvatu.
(ka-u) taṉṉip pukaḻntu pukaḻpeṟa niṉittal nīr ūṟṟit tī vaḷarttatu pōlākum.
piṟar matippuvēṇṭuvāṉ ceyyat takkavi
piṟarāl peruñcuṭṭu vēṇṭuvāṉ yāṇṭum
maṟavāmē nōṟpatoṉ ṟuṇṭu - piṟarpiṟar
cīrellān tūṟṟic ciṟumi puṟaṅkāttu
yāryārkkum tāḻcci colal 20
20. For him who desires much notice from others, there is one act of penance which he must never forget to practise; continually to dwell upon all the merits, and conceal the defects, of others and to address all with humility.
(pa-u) piṟarāl perum cuṭṭu vēṇṭuvāṉ - piṟarāl matikkappaṭum periya matippi vēṇṭukiṉṟavaṉ; yāṇṭum maṟavāmē nōṟpatu oṉṟu uṇṭu-eppoḻutum maṟavāmal ceyvatu oṉṟu uḷḷatu; (atu) piṟar piṟar cīr ellām tūṟṟi - maṟṟavaratu ciṟappukkaḷi yellām collip parappi; ciṟumi puṟam kāttu - ciṟappallātavaṟṟip paravāmal aṭakki; yār yārkkum tāḻcci colal - ellāriṭattum vaṇakkamāṉa moḻikaḷip pēcutal.
(ka-u) piṟar matippi virumpukiṉṟavaṉ maṟṟavar naṉmikaḷic collit tīmikaḷic collātirukka vēṇṭum. ellāriṭattum vaṇakkamāṉa moḻikaḷip pēca vēṇṭum.
kaṟṟapaṭi nillātavariṉ coṟcāturiyam
kaṟṟup piṟarkkurittut tāmnillār vāyppaṭūum
veṟṟurik kuṇṭōr valiyuṭimi - coṟṟanīr
nillāta teṉṉeṉṟu nāṇuṟippa nērttoruvaṉ
collāmē cūḻntu colal 21
21. The empty speech of those, who do not themselves persist in what they have learned, and tught to others has weight only in one respect; in the indirect reproof of those who do not put them to shame by asking directly, why is it that you, who have tught others, do not practise yourself what you teach.
(pa-u) kaṟṟu piṟarkku urittu tām nillār vāypaṭūum - (nūlkaḷi) paṭittu ak karuttukkaḷip piṟarkkuc collit tām avvāṟu naṭavātavaruṭiya vāyiliruntu piṟakkum; veṟu urikku ōr vali uṭimi uṇṭu - payaṉillāta collukku oru valimi uḷḷatu; (atu) coṟṟa nīrnillātatu eṉ eṉṟu - (nāṅkaḷ) naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṉṟa nīr appaṭi naṭavātatu ēṉ eṉṟu; nāṇ uṟippa nērttu oruvaṉ collāmē cūḻntu colal - nāṇam mika uṇṭākumpaṭi oruvaṉ nērē collātapaṭi ārāyntu colvatākum.
(ka-u) kaṟṟapaṭi tāṉ naṭakkātavar piṟarkkup pōtippatu pētimi.
poruḷpeṟṟuk kalvi kaṟpittalākātu
piṟarkkup payaṉpaṭat tāṅkaṟṟa viṟpār
tamakkup payaṉvē ṟuṭiyār - tiṟappaṭūun
tīviṉi yañcā viṟalkoṇṭu teṉpulattār
kōviṉi vēli koḷal. 22
22. Those, who sell to others the advantages of the mysteries which they have learned, reserve an advantage of another description to themselves; the receiving the labours of the king of the dead, with the hardihood that defies the terrors of an evil life.
(pa-u) piṟarkkup payaṉpaṭa tām kaṟṟa viṟpār - piṟarukkup payaṉākumpaṭi tām kaṟṟavaṟṟip poruḷukkuk koṭuppavar; tamakku payaṉvēṟu uṭiyar - tamakku vēṟoru payaṉi uṭiyavarāvar; (atu) tiṟam paṭūum tīviṉi añcā viṟal koṇṭu teṉpulattār kōviṉi - palavakippaṭṭa tīya ceyalkaḷiyum ceyya añcāta vīram koṇṭa teṉpulattār eṉṉum pitirkaḷiṉ aracaṉi; vēli koḷal - (taṅkaḷi narakattiliṭṭu varuttutalākiya) vēliyic ceyyap paṇṇutal.
(ka-u) poruḷ peṟṟuk koṇṭu kalviyic collik koṭuttavar narakil varuntuvar.
kaṟṟavar capiyil kallātār eḻutal
kaṟpaṉa ūḻaṟṟār kalvik kaḻakattāṅ(ku)
oṟkamiṉ ṟūttivāy aṅkāttal - maṟṟuttam
valluru añcaṉmiṉ eṉpavē māpaṟavi
pulluru añcuva pōl. 23
23. The ceaseless chattering, in the learned assembly, of the coarse lips of those who were not destined to learning, tells us not to fear their otherwise powerful appearance, as the beasts and birds fear a figure of straw.
(pa-u) kaṟpaṉa ūḻ aṟṟār - kaṟkavēṇṭiyavaṟṟik kaṟkum nalla ūḻ illātavar; kalvi kaḻakattu - kaṟṟavar irukkum capiyil; oṟkam iṉṟu ūttivāy aṅkāttal - aṭakkam illāmal ūtti vāyit tiṟantu pēcutal; māpaṟavi puluru añcuva pōl valuru añcaṉ miṉ eṉpavē - vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum pullāṟceytu vikkappaṭṭa uruvattukku añcuvatu pōlat tammuṭiya valimiyāṉa uruvattukku añca vēṇṭām eṉṟu colvatu pōlākum.
(ka-u) kaṟṟavar capiyil kallātavar eḻuntāl avarik kaṟṟavar poruṭpaṭuttār.
taṟperumiyāḷariṭam kalvi cērātu
pōkkaṟu kalvi pulamikkār pālaṉṟi
mīkkoḷ nakiyiṉār vāyccērā - tākkaṇaṅkum
āṇavām peṇmi yuṭitteṉiṉum peṇṇalam
pēṭukoḷap paṭuva til. 24
24. Inexhustible learning is attinable only by those who excel in observation; not by those who smile in vin self-conceit. Though a woman posses charms which Lachmi might envy, they cannot be enjoyed by an Hermaphrodite.
(pa-u) tākku aṇaṅkum āṇ avām peṇmi uṭittu eṉiṉum - (peṇ) tākki varuttukiṉṟatum āṇkaḷāl virumpappaṭuvatu mākiya peṇmit taṉmi uṭiyatāyiṉum; peṇ nalam pēṭu koḷap paṭuvatil - peṇṇiṉuṭiya iṉpam āṇ taṉmi illātavaṉāl koḷḷappaṭuvatilli; (atupōla) pōkku aṟu kalvi - kuṟṟamaṟṟa kalvi; pulam mikkārpāl aṉṟi - aṟivu mikkavariṭattilallāmal; mīk koḷ nakiyiṉār vāy cērā - taṟperumiyāl nakiyāṭukiṉṟa variṭattiṟ cēramāṭṭā.
(ka-u) kalvi taṟperumi koṇṭiruppavari aṭiyātu.
mūṭarkku aṟivu koḷutta muṭiyātu
kaṟṟaṉa kallār cevimāṭṭik kiyuṟūuṅ
kuṟṟan tamatē piṟitaṉṟu - muṟṟuṇarntun
tāmavar taṉmi yuṇarātār tammuṇarā
ētilari nōva tevaṉ. 25
25. If learned men incur disrespect, while forcing instruction into the ears of the ignorant, the fault is their own. If, with all their knowledge, they do not know their neighbours’ qualities, why should they be hurt at these ignorant neighbours for not knowing their merits.
(pa-u) kaṟṟaṉa - tām kaṟṟavaṟṟi; kallār cevimāṭṭi - mūṭariṉ ceviyil nuḻikka muyalvatāl; kiyuṟūum kuṟṟam tamatē - aṭiyum kuṟṟam avaruṭiyatē; piṟitu aṉṟu - vēṟoruvaruṭiyataṉṟu; muṟṟum uṇarntum avar taṉmi uṇarātār - ellām aṟintum ammūṭariṉ taṉmiyi aṟiyātavar; tam uṇarā ētilari nōvatu evaṉ - tamatu kuṟṟatti uṇarātu ammūṭari nontu koḷvatu ēṉ?
(ka-u) mūṭarukkuk kalvi pukaṭṭappukuntu avarkaḷāl avamāṉappaṭṭa kaṟṟavarkaḷ ellām aṟintiruntum ammūṭariṉ taṉmiyi aṟiyātavarāvar. avarkaḷ tamatu kuṟṟattukku varuntātu ammūṭari nontukoḷvatu ēṉ?
kīḻōr mēlōr iyalpu
vēttavi kāvār mikaṉmakkaḷ vēṟucilar
kāttatu koṇṭāṅ kuvappeytār - māttakiya
antap purattatu pūñi puṟaṅkaṭiya
kantukol pūṭkik kaḷiṟu. 26
26. The noble wait not in attendance at the royal court: and there are some others, who have no enjoyment in waiting there. The cat frequents the splendid zenana, while the hollow handed elephants, which can tear up the pillars to which they are bound, remin without the gate.
(pa-u) mikal makkaḷ vēttu avi kāvār - uyarntavarkaḷ aracaṉuṭiya capiyil (parivāraṅkaḷākak) kāttuk koṇṭirukka māṭṭārkaḷ; vēṟu cilar kāttatu koṇṭu āṅku uvappu eytār - ivarkaḷallāta vēṟu cilar (avvaraca capiyik) kāttuk koṇṭu niṉṟum makiḻcciyaṭiya māṭṭār; mā takiya antappurattatu pūñi - perumiyuṭiya peṇkaḷ taṅkum aṟiyiṭattup pūṉi irukkum; kantu kol pūṭki kaḷiṟu puṟaṅkaṭiyatu - kaṭṭut taṟiyi muṟikkum valiyuṭiya yāṉi talivāyilil niṟkum.
vēntu - vēttu eṉa niṉṟatu.
(ka-u) aracaratu parivāraṅkaḷāka iruntum cilar perumi aṭiyār. avvāṟillāta aṟiñar veḷiyē niṟpiṉum perumi aṭivar.
ivarkku ivar teyvameṉal
kulamakaṭkut teyvam koḻunaṉē maṉṟa
putalvarkkut tantiyun tāyum - aṟavōrk
kaṭikaḷē teyvam aṉivōrkkun teyvam
ilimukap pimpūṇ iṟi. 27
27. To well-born women, their husband is a god; to children, their father and mother; to the virtuous, their spiritual preceptor is a god; and a god to all, is the kind, adorned with leaf-like ornaments of pale gold.
(pa-u) kulam makaṭku koḻunaṉē teyvam - nalla kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta (oḻukkamuṭiya) peṇkaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉē teyvam; putalvarkku tantiyum tāyum (teyvam) - makkaḷukkut tantiyum tāyum teyvam; aṟavōrkku aṭikaḷē (teyvam) - (nallavaḻiyil cellum) māṇāk karukku āciriyarē teyvam; ellārkkum ilimukam pimpūṇ iṟi teyvam - ellōrukkum ilimukam pōṉṟa pacumiyāṉa poṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷ aṇinta aracaṉ teyvamāvāṉ; maṉṟa - uṟutiyāka.
(ka-u) kulamakaṭkuk kaṇavaṉum, putalvarkkut tantiyum tāyum, māṇākkarukku āciriyarum, kuṭikaḷ ellōrukkum aracaṉum teyvamāvar.
kuṭikaḷik kāvāta āṭci payaṉaṟṟatu
kaṇṇiṟ colicceviyiṉ nōkkum iṟimāṭci
puṇṇiyattiṉ pālatē yāyiṉun - taṇṇaḷiyāṉ
maṉpati yōmpātārk keṉṉum vayappaṭimaṟ
ṟeṉpayakkum āṇal lavarkku. 28
28. Though men possess the royal privilege of speaking with the eye, and seeing with the ear, as the fruit of former virtues; of what avil is it, unless they protect the earth with their clemency? What advantage will mighty weapons afford to those, who are wanting in manly courage?
(pa-u) kaṇṇiṉ coli ceviyil nōkkum iṟimāṭci - kaṇ pārviyāl karutti aṟivittuk kātiṉāl kēṭpatāl kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭatu pōl aṟiyum aracaṉuṭiya perumi; puṇṇiyattiṉ pālatē āyiṉum - muṟceyta puṇṇiyattāl uṇṭāvatāṉālum; taṇ aḷiyāl maṉpati ōmpātārkku - kuḷirnta karuṇiyāl kuṭikaḷip pātukāvāta aracarukku; eṉṉum - (avaruṭiya ciṟappu) eṉṉa payaṉuṭiyatu? āṇ allavarkku vayappaṭi eṉ payakkum - āṇ taṉmiyillātavarkku veṟṟi tarattakka āyutam eṉṉa payaṉit tarum?
(ka-u) kuṭikaḷi nalla muṟiyil kāppāṟṟāviṭṭāl araca atikāram payaṉaṟṟatu.
kuṭikaḷi varutti vari koḷḷalākātu
kuṭikoṉ ṟiṟikoḷḷuṅ kōmakaṟkuk kaṟṟā
maṭikoṉṟu pālkoḷalum māṇpē - kuṭiyōmpik
koḷḷumā koḷvōrkkuk kāṇṭumē mānitiyam
veḷḷattiṉ mēlum pala. 29
29. To a prince, who destroys his subjects to obtin his revenue, to cut open the udder of a new milched cow, to obtin her milk, would be comparatively creditable; but to those who protect their subjects before receiving their own dues, we have seen wealth overflowing like a flood.
(pa-u) kuṭi koṉṟu iṟi koḷḷum kōmakaṟku - kuṭikaḷi varutti varivāṅkum aracaṉukku; kaṉṟu ā maṭikoṉṟu pāl koḷalum māṇpē - kaṉṟuṭiya pacuviṉ maṭiyi aṟuttup pāl eṭuppatum nalla ceyalākum; kuṭi ōmpi koḷḷumā koḷvōrkku - kuṭikaḷip pātukāttu vāṅkum muṟimiyil (variyi) vāṅkum aracarukku; mānitiyam veḷḷattiṉ mēlum pala kāṇṭum -periya celvam veḷḷattiṟkum atikamākap perukuvatik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
(ka-u) kuṭikaḷi varuttātu varikoḷḷum aracaṉiṉ
celvam vaḷarum.
koṭuṅkōl aracaṉ iyalpu
iṉṟu koḷaṟpāla nāḷik koḷappoṟāṉ
niṉṟu kuṟiyirappa nērpaṭāṉ - ceṉṟoruvaṉ
āvaṉa kūṟiṉ eyiṟalippāṉ āṟalikkum
vēṭalaṉ vēntum alaṉ. 30
30. He, who will not wait till to-morrow, to receive, that which becomes due to-day; who will not attend, when one stands to beg redress; and who will gnash his teeth with impatience when one may approach to offer wise advice; is neither a Vedan who robs on the highway, nor a king.
(pa-u) iṉṟu koḷaṟpāla nāḷi koḷap poṟāṉ - iṉṟu vāṅkavēṇṭiya poruḷi nāḷikku vāṅkap poṟuttirukka māṭṭāṉ; niṉṟu kuṟi irappa nērpaṭāṉ - (kuṭikaḷ) muṉṉē niṉṟu tamatu kuṟikaḷic colla akappaṭamāṭṭāṉ; oruvaṉ ceṉṟu āvaṉa kūṟa eyiṟu alippāṉ - oruvaṉ ceṉṟu taṉakku ākavēṇṭiyavaṟṟic coṉṉāl pallik kaṭittuk kōpippāṉ; āṟu alikkum vēṭu alaṉ vēntum alaṉ - (avaṉ) vaḻippaṟi ceyyum vēṭaṉumallaṉ aracaṉum allaṉ.
(ka-u) kuṭikaḷi varuttip poruḷ koḷḷum aracaṉ vaḻipaṟikkum vēṭaṉilum koṭiyavaṉ.
araca celvattil mayakkam
muṭippa muṭittuppiṉ pūcuva pūci
uṭuppa uṭuttuṇpa uṇṇā - iṭittiṭittuk
kaṭṭuri kūṟiṟ cevikoḷā kaṇviḻiyā
neṭṭuyirppō ṭuṟṟa piṇam. 31
31. A king, who after crowning his head with chaplets, rubbing his body with unguents, clothing himself in rich robes, and feasting on rich viands, will not lend his ear, nor open his eyes, though addressed with reiterated earnestness, is but a corpse endued with breath.
(pa-u) muṭippa muṭittu - muṭiyiṟ cūṭattakka (pūmālikaḷic) cūṭikkoṇṭu; piṉ pūcuva pūci - piṉpu uṭaliṟ pūcavēṇṭiya (puṉuku cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟip) pūcik koṇṭu; uṭuppa uṭuttu - uṭukka vēṇṭiya (uṭikaḷi) uṭuttuk koṇṭu; uṇpa uṇṇā - uṇṇattakka (nalla uṇavukaḷi) uṇṭu koṇṭu, iṭittu iṭittu kaṭṭuri kūṟiṉ - palamuṟi nerukki uṟuti moḻikaḷic coṉṉālum; cevi kēḷā - kātiṉāṟ kēḷāmalum; kaṇ viḻiyā - kaṇkaḷāṟ pārāmalum (irukkiṉṟa aracar); neṭṭuyirppōṭu uṟṟa piṇam - mūccu viṭukiṉṟa piṇaṅkaḷāvar.
muṭittup pūci uṭuttu uṇṭu kēḷātu pārātu irukkum aracar mūccuviṭum piṇattukkuc camam.
(ka-u) araca celvattil mayaṅkik kuṭikaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷik kēḷāta aracaṉ piṇattukkuc camam.
aracaṉ ceyyavēṇṭiyavi
ōṟṟiṟ ṟeriyā ciṟippuṟat tōrtumeṉap
poṟṟōḷ tuṇiyāt teritantum - kuṟṟam
aṟivariteṉ ṟañcuvatē ceṅkōṉmi ceṉṟu
kuṟiyiṭiṉuṅ kēḷāmi yaṉṟu. 32
32. The duty of him who holds a sceptre. is to fear on account of the difficulty of detecting wrong; even though, assured that it cannot be ascertined by spies, he has in person made private inquiry, his own arm adorned with gold being his only protection; not to refuse to listen, though men come and complin of it.
(pa-u) oṟṟil teriyā - oṟṟarāl aṟiya muṭiyātavikaḷi; ciṟippuṟattu ōrtum eṉa - otukkiṭaṅkaḷil niṉṟu aṟivōmeṉṟu; poṉ tōḷ tuṇiyā teritantum - poṉvaḷi aṇinta (taṉatu) tōḷē taṉakkut tuṇiyāka aṅku niṉṟu aṟintum; kuṟṟam aṟivu aritu eṉṟu añcuvatē ceṅkōṉmi - kuṟṟam yātu eṉṟu aṟiya muṭiyavilliyē eṉṟu aracaṉ payappaṭuvatē nīti āṭciyākum; ceṉṟu kuṟiyiṭiṉum kēḷāmi aṉṟu - ceṉṟu kuṟikaḷi muṟiyiṭṭālum kēḷāmal iruppataṉṟu.
(ka-u) aracaṉuṭiya ciṟappiyalpu kuṭikaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷi aṟintu avikaḷit tīrttalākum.
poy nīkki mey koḷḷa vēṇṭum
ētilār yātum pukala iṟimakaṉ
kōtorīik koḷki mutukkuṟivu - nērniṉṟu
kākki veḷiteṉpār eṉcollār tāykkoli
cālpuṭit teṉpāru muṇṭu. 33
33. While others assert every thing, the wisdom of the Prince is to receive their assertions, rejecting what is false. What will not men say, who standing opposite to a crow, will call it white? There are some, who will even say, that the murder of a mother is noble.
(pa-u) ētilār yātum pukala - piṟar tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟu ellām colla; iṟimakaṉ kōtuorīi koḷki - aracaṉ avarkaḷ kūṟiya vaṟṟiṉ kuṟṟamāṉavi nīkki nallavaṟṟik koḷḷutal; mutukkuṟivu - aṟivuṭimiyākum; nēr niṉṟu kākki veḷiteṉpār eṉ colār - kākattukku muṉṉāl niṉṟu atu veṇṇiṟamuṭiya teṉṟu collu kiṉṟavarkaḷ etic colla māṭṭārkaḷ; tāykkoli cālpu uṭittu eṉpārum uṇṭu - tāyik koli ceytal nīti eṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟavarkaḷum ivvulakattil uṇṭu.
(ka-u) piṟar kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟiṟ poyn nīkki uṇmi koḷḷutal aracarkku aṟivuṭimi.
mūṭar kēṭaṟiyār
kaṇkūṭāp paṭṭatu kēṭeṉiṉuṅ kīḻmakkaṭ(ku)
uṇṭō vuṇarccimaṟ ṟillākum - maṇṭeri
tāṉvāy maṭuppiṉum mācuṇam kaṇṭuyilva
pērā perumūc ceṟintu 34
34. Though ruin be before their eyes, will fools perceive it? No, they will not: though thick flames envelope their body, the sleeping rock serpents will not arouse from their loud-breathing slumbers.
(pa-u) kēṭu kaṇkūṭu ā paṭṭatu eṉiṉum - (tamakku) varuṅkēṭu kaṇṇukkut teripavāyiṉum; kīḻmakkaṭku uṇṭō uṇarcci - mūṭarukku ataṉiṉṟu tappippiḻikkum uṇarcci uḷḷatō? illākum - illi yākum; maṇṭu eritāṉ vāy maṭuppiṉum - mikka nerupput taṉṉic cūḻn tālum; mācuṇam perumūcceṟintu kaṇ tuyilva - pāmpu perumūccu viṭṭu nittiri koḷḷum; pērā - avviṭattiviṭṭu nīṅkātu.
(ka-u) āpattu aṇmiyil iruppati aṟintālum kīḻmakkaḷ ataṉi uṇarār.
kēṭṭukku aṟikuṟi
naṭpup pirittal pakinaṭṭal oṟṟikaḻtal
pakkattār yāriyum aiyuṟutal takkār
neṭumoḻi kōṟal kuṇampiṟi tātal
keṭuvatu kāṭṭuṅ kuṟi. 35
35. The estrangement of friends, the receiving of enemies into friendship; to scoff at a spy, and to suspect all associates; to despise the continued advice of the wise; and become changed in disposition, are signs of approaching ruin.
(pa-u) naṭpup pirittal - naṇparkaḷip pirintu pōkumpaṭi ceytalum; paki naṭṭal - pakivari naṇparākkik koḷḷutalum; oṟṟu ikaḻtal - oṟṟari avamatittalum; pakkattār yāriyum aiyuṟutal - kūṭa iruppārkaḷ takkār neṭumo kōṟal - aṟivuṭiyavariṉ ellāri yum cantēkittalum; aṟivurikaḷi aḻittalum; kuṇam piṟitu ātal - nallakuṇattiliruntu māṟupaṭutal; keṭuvatu kāṭṭum kuṟi - (ākiya ivikaḷ) varappōkum kēṭṭik kāṭṭum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷākum.
(ka-u) naṭpup pirittal mutaliyaṉa kēṭu varuvataṟ kaṟikuṟikaḷākum.
celvac cerukkāl periyārip piḻittal
paṇiyap paṭuvār puṟaṅkaṭiya rākat
taṇivil kaḷippiṉāl tāḻvārk - kaṇiya(tu)
iḷiyāḷ muyakkeṉiṉuñ cēyttaṉṟē mūttāḷ
toliyāta 1pōkaṅ koḷal. 36
36. The men, who are sunk in immoderate delights, while those who ought to be honoured are waiting at the gate, though now they embrace the goddess of prosperity, are not far from the enjoyment of the charms of the close bosom of her elder sister, adversity.
(pa-u) paṇiyappaṭuvār puṟaṅkaṭiyar āka - tām vaṇaṅki etirkoḷḷattakkavar talivāyilil niṟka; taṇivu il kaḷippiṉāl tāḻvārkku - aṭaṅkāta celvac cerukkiṉāl (avari etir koḷḷa) kālam nīṭṭippavarukku; iḷiyāḷ muyakku aṇiyatu eṉiṉum - ilakkumiyiṉ cērkki avarkkuk kiṭṭa irunta pōtum; mūttāḷ toliyāta pōkam koḷal cēyttu aṉṟu - mūtēviyiṉ nīṅkāta cērkkiyip peṟutal tolivil illi.
(ka-u) celvac cerukkiṉāl periyāri matiyāviṭṭāl mūtēvi kuṭi koḷvaḷ.
naṟkuṇamuṭiyōr paṇpu
kaṇṇōk karumpā nakimukamē nāṇmalarā
iṉmoḻiyiṉ vāymiyē tīṅkāyā - vaṇmi
palamā nalaṅkaṉinta paṇpuṭiyā raṉṟē
caliyāta kaṟpa taru. 37
37. The nobly generous whose glance is a bud; their smiling countenance, a fresh blown blossom; the truth of their courteous speech, the sweet young fruit; and munificence, the ripe fruit; are not these the real inexhustible calpa tree?
(pa-u) kaṇ nōkku arumpu ā - kaṇṇāl pārkkum pārvi arumpākavum; naki mukamē nāḷ malar ā - malarnta mukamē putup pūvākavum; iṉmoḻiyiṉ vāymiyē tīm kāy ā - iṉiya colliṉ uṇmiyē iṉippāṉa kāyākavum; vaṇmi palam ā - koṭiyē paḻam ākavum; nalam kaṉinta paṇpuṭiyār aṉṟē caliyāta kaṟpaka taru - naṉmikaḷ paḻutta naṟkuṇamuṭiyavar allavō iḷittal illāta kaṟpaka maramāvar.
(ka-u) niṟinta kuṇamuṭiyavar collālum ceyalkaḷālum iṉiyarāyiruppar.
yāṉiyuṇaviṉ oruciṟitu kōṭi eṟumpiṟku uṇavu
vāṅkuṅ kavaḷat toruciṟitu vāytappiṉ
tūṅkuṅ kaḷiṟō tuyaruṟā - āṅkatukoṇ(ṭu)
ūrum eṟumpiṅ korukōṭi yuyyumāl
āruṅ kiḷiyō ṭayiṉṟu. 38
38. The drowsy elephant will not grudge the few grins which may fall from the ball of rice that it is receiving: the creeping ants will pick them up, and ten million living creatures will subsist, feasting with their crowded progeny.
(pa-u) vāṅkum kavaḷattu oru ciṟitu vāy tappiṉ - tāṉ vāṅki uṇkiṉṟa oruvāy uṇaviṉ oru ciṟu pakuti vāykkuḷ pōkātu tavaṟi viḻuntuviṭṭāl; tūṅkum kaḷiṟu ō tuyar uṟātu - acintu koṇṭu niṟkum yāṉiyō tuṉpam aṭiyamāṭṭātu; āṅku atu koṇṭu - avviṭattil viḻunta ataṉik koṇṭu; iṅku ūrum eṟumpu oru kōṭi ārum kiḷiyōṭu ayiṉṟu uyyum - ivvulakil ūrukiṉṟa eṟumpukaḷil oru kōṭi taṅkaḷ niṟinta cuṟṟattōṭu uṇavi uṇṭu uyir vāḻum.
(ka-u) yāṉikkuk koṭukkum kavaḷa uṇaviṉ oru ciṟitu kōṭi eṟumpukku uṇavākum.
aracaṉuṭiya naṉ matippu
mākañ ciṟukak kuvittu nitikkuvi
īkiyiṉ ēkkaḻuttam mikkuṭiya - mākol
pakimukatta veḷvēlāṉ pārviyiṟ ṟīṭṭum
nakimukatta naṉku matippu. 39
39. The smiling approbation, which is expressed in the glance of the prince, whose bright javelin threatens the death of wild beasts; is a prouder thing, than the gift of a heap of treasure, large enough to diminish the sky.
(pa-u) paki mukatta mā kol veḷ vēlāṉ - pakivaratu pōrkkaḷattē kutiriyik kolkiṉṟa veḷḷiya vēliyuṭiya aracaṉ; naki mukatta pārviyil tīṭṭum naṉku matippu - malarnta mukattiluḷḷa kaṇkaḷāl tōṟṟuvikkum naṉmatippukaḷ; mākam ciṟuka kuvitta nitikkuvi īkiyiṉ - mēkamum ciṟitu (eṉṉumpaṭi) kuvitta poruḷ kuviyalik koṭuttaliṉum pārkka; ēkkaḻuttam mikkuṭiya- perumitam periyatākum.
(ka-u) aracaṉ naṉmatippip peṟutal poṉ kuviyalip peṟutalilum perumi taruvatu.
aṭikkalam kāttal
kaḷikaṇāt tammaṭintārk kuṟṟuḻiyum maṟṟōr
viḷivuṉṉi veṟṟuṭampu tāṅkār - taḷarnaṭiya(tu)
ūṉuṭam peṉṟu pukaḻuṭam pōmputaṟkē
tāṉuṭam paṭṭārka ṭām. 40
40. Those will not spare their worthless bodies, in the hope of any future profit, should anything happen even to such as are under their protection; who, sensible how fril is the body of flesh, have determined to preserve the body of glory.
(pa-u) ūṉ uṭampu taḷar naṭiyatu eṉṟu - iṟicciyālāṉa ivvuṭampu nilitaḷarum iyalpuṭiyatu eṉṟu karuti; pukaḻ uṭampu ōmputaṟku uṭampaṭṭārkaḷ tām - pukaḻ uṭampi vaḷarppataṟkut tīrmāṉittavarkaḷ; tam kaḷikaṇ ā aṭintārkku-tamatu ātaravināṭi vantu cērntavarkku; uṟṟu uḻiyum - (oru ciṟiya tuṉpam) vantaviṭattum; maṟṟu ōr viḷivu uṉṉi veṟṟi uṭampu tāṅkār - (pukaḻallāta) vēṟoṉṟaṉi virumpit tamatu ūṉuṭampi vittirukkamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) ulakil tampukaḻi nāṭṭa virumpuvōr tamatu uṭalik koṭuttum tammi aṭikkalamāka vantaṭintavarik kāppar.
matippi iḻattalākātu
tammuṭi yāṟṟalum māṉamun tōṟṟuttam
iṉṉuyir ōmpiṉum ōmpuka - piṉṉarc
ciṟuvari yāyiṉum maṉṟa tamakkāṅ(ku)
iṟuvari yilli yeṉiṉ. 41
41. If any would save their sweet lives, by sacrificing courage and honour; let them to do so, provided even then they can be secure from death, for ever so short a time.
(pa-u) piṉṉar ciṟuvari āyiṉum maṉṟa tamakku iṟuvari illi eṉiṉ - iṉimēl ciṟitu kāla aḷavāyiṉum tamakkut tiṇṇamākac cāvu illiyāṉāl; tammuṭi āṟṟalum māṉamum tōṟṟu - tamatu valimiyiyum matippiyum iḻantu; tam iṉṉuyir ōmpiṉum ōmpuka - tamatu iṉiya uyirik kāppāṟṟiṉum kāppāṟṟuka. ‘āṅku’ iṭiccol.
(ka-u) iṉimēl maraṇamilliyeṉṟu kaṇṭāl taṉatu māṉattiyum matippiyum oruvaṉ iḻantāyiṉum taṉ uyirik kāppāṟṟalām.
cāvukku añcātavar
kalaṉaḻinta kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirum aintu
pulaṉoṭuṅkap poyyoḻin tārum - koliñāṭpiṉ
moympuṭi vīrarum añcār muraṇmaṟali
tumpi muṭicū ṭiṉum. 42
42. Virtuous matrons, who have lid aside their ornaments in widowhood; those, who, having done away with falsehood, have brought the five senses to agree: and warriors, who, in the slughterous field of battle, retin their courage; fear not, though grim Yaman assume the chaplet of Drona flowers.
(pa-u) kalaṉ aḻinta kaṟpuṭi peṇṭirum - kaṇavaṉ iṟattalāl nakikaḷik kaḷinta kaṟpuṭiya peṇkaḷum; aintu pulaṉ oṭuṅka poy oḻintārum - (mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi eṉṉum) aintu pulaṉkaḷum kuṉṟumpaṭi poyyi viṭṭavarum; koli ñāṭpiṉ moympu uṭi vīrarum - pōrkkaḷattiluḷḷa valiya vīrarum; muraṇ maṟali tumpi cūṭiṉum añcār - valiya iyamaṉ taṉatu muṭiyilē tumpi māli cūṭiṉālum (etirttālum) añcamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) kaṇavaṉi iḻanta kaṟpuṭi makaḷirum, tuṟavikaḷum, vīrarum uyiriviṭa añcār.
meyyaṟivuṭiyōr cāka añcār
puḻuneḷintu puṇṇaḻuki yōcaṉi nāṟum
kaḻimuṭi nāṟṟatta vēṉum - viḻalar
viḷivuṉṉi veytuyirppar meyppayaṉ koṇṭār
cuḷiyār cumipōṭu taṟku 43
43. Fools will sigh deeply at the thought of death, though their bodies be corrupted with sores full of crawling worms, the rank odour of which may be perceived at the distance of five miles; but those, who have derived the true advantage of the body, will not shudder to lay down the burden.
(pa-u) puṇ aḻuki puḻu neḷintu yōcaṉi nāṟum kaḻimuṭi nāṟṟatta ēṉum - (tamatu uṭal) puṇṇāṉatu aḻukip puḻu neḷintu oru yōcaṉi tūram mikutiyāka vīcukiṉṟa tīya nāṟṟam uṭiyatāyirunta pōtilum; viḻalar - aṟivillātavar; viḷivu uṉṉi veytu uyirppar - iṟattali añci perumūccu viṭuvar; mey payaṉ koṇṭār - uṭam piṉālākiya payaṉi aṭinta aṟivuṭiyōr; cumi pōṭutaṟku cuḷiyār - uṭampākiya cumiyi viṭutaṟku naṭuṅkamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) uṭampiṉ payaṉākiya meyyaṟivip peṟṟōr cākap payappaṭār.
aracaṉ evvaḻi kuṭikaḷu mavvaḻi
ikaḻi ṉikaḻntāṅ kiṟimakaṉ oṉṟu
pukaḻiṉum okkap pukaḻpa - ikaṉmaṉṉaṉ
cīrvaḻip paṭṭatē maṉpatimaṟ ṟeṉceyum
nīrvaḻip paṭṭa puṇi. 44
44. If the prince speak contemptuously, they will speak so likewise; and if he prise, they will prise in equal terms, thus the world are led according to the taste of the warrior king. What else does the boat, that is borne along the current of the water?
(pa-u) iṟimakaṉ oṉṟu ikaḻiṉ - aracaṉ oṉṟi ikaḻntāl; (okka) ikaḻntu - (kuṭikaḷum) tāmumkūṭa ati ikaḻntu; pukaḻiṉum okka pukaḻpa - (aracaṉ) pukaḻntāl tāmum kūṭap pukaḻvar; puṇi nīr vaḻippaṭṭa - teppam nīr cellum vaḻi cellum; maṉpati ikal maṉṉaṉ cīr vaḻippaṭṭatē - (atupōla) makkaṭ kūṭṭamum valiya aracaṉiṉ ciṟanta vaḻiyilēyē cellum; maṟṟu eṉ ceyum - (avvāṟu ceyvatit tavira) vēṟu eṉṉa ceyya muṭiyum?
(ka-u) aracaṉ āṇippaṭiyē kuṭikaḷum naṭakka nēriṭum.
nal amiccar iyalpu
cevicuṭac ceṉṟāṅ kiṭittaṟivu mūṭṭi
vekuḷiṉum vāyverīip pērā - kaviḻmatatta
kimmā vayattatō pākumaṟ ṟettiṟattum
ammāṇ piṉavē amiccu. 45
45. The keeper approaches the elephants when liquid flows from its temples in the season of frantic violence; and, as he perfects its discipline by repeated blows until its ear burns with pin, however enraged it be, flinches not in fear, but keeps the charge: such is every respect is the duty of the King’s minister.
(pa-u) pāku - (yāṉiyic celuttum) pākaṉ; ettiṟattum kaviḻ matatta kimā vayattatō - evvaḷavum matam coriyum yāṉiyi aṭakkātu ataṟku aṭaṅkuvāṉō? aṭaṅkāṉ; (atu pōl) amiccu - mantiri; (ettiṟattum) ammāṇ piṉavē - evvakiyilum at taṉmiyarē āvar; ceṉṟu - (aracaṉ neṟi tavaṟiyaviṭattu avaṉiṭam) pōy; cevi cuṭa iṭittu aṟivu mūṭṭi - (avaṉatu) cevikaḷ varuntum paṭi iṭitturittu aṟivu uṇṭākac ceytu; vekuḷiṉum vāy verīi pērā - kōpittālum (avaṉukkup) payantu nīṅkār. ‘āṅku’ aci.
(ka-u) aracaṉ kōpittālum avaṉi viṭṭu nīṅkātu amiccar avaṉukku nallaṟivu pukaṭṭuvar.
aracaṉiṭam camayamaṟintu pēcavēṇṭum
kivarum vēntaṉ namakkeṉṟu kātalitta
cevvi teriyā turiyaṟka - ovvorukāl
eṇmiya ṉēṉum ariyaṉ peritammā
kaṇṇilaṉ uḷveyarppiṉāṉ. 46
46. Utter not all you desire, without observing the proper season, in the belief that the king is under your influence; though occasionally he may be easy to manage; yet how difficult is he when destitute of mercy, and burning with inward rage?
(pa-u) vēntaṉ namakku kivarum eṉṟu - aracaṉ namakku iṇaṅki varuvāṉ eṉṟu niṉittu; cevvi teriyātu kātalitta uriyaṟka - camayam teriyāmal (tām) virumpiyatic collātiruttal vēṇṭum; ovvorukāl eṇmiyaṉ ēṉum - ciṟcila camayaṅkaḷil (kēṭṭa vaṟṟip peṟutaṟku) eḷiyaṉpōl tōṉṟiṉālum; peritu ariyaṉ kaṇ ilaṉ uḷ veyarppiṉāṉ - avaṉ (oṉṟik koṭuttaṟkum kāṇṭaṟkum) mika ariyaṉ irakkamillātavaṉ uḷkōpa muḷḷavaṉ.
(ka-u) aracaṉiṭam camayamaṟintu pēci virumpiyavaṟṟik kēṭṭup peṟutal vēṇṭum.
aracar iyalpu
paḻami kaṭippiṭiyār kēṇmiyum pārār
kiḻami piṟitoṉṟuṅ koḷḷār - vekuḷiṉmaṉ
kātaṉmi yuṇṭō iṟimāṇṭārk kētilarum
ārvalarum illi avarkku. 47
47. They remember not old acquaintance; they regard not intimacy, they acknowledge not the ties of blood, or other connexion; what affection have princes in their rage? They know neither foes nor friends.
(pa-u) vekuḷiṉ maṉ - (aracar) mikak kōpittāl; paḻami kaṭippiṭiyār - paḻiya paḻakkattip paṟṟi niṉiyār; kēṇmiyum pārār - naṇpar eṉpatiyum nōkkār; piṟitu - iviyallāmal: kiḻami oṉṟum koḷḷār - ivar namakku uriyavar eṉpatic ciṟitum niṉiyār; iṟi māṇṭārkku kātaṉmi uṇṭō - araciyalil āḻntavarkku aṉpu eṉa oṉṟu uṇṭō; avarkku ētilarum ārvalarum ilar - avarkaḷukku ayalāreṉavum cuṟṟattāreṉavum kiṭiyātu.
(ka-u) aracar kōpittāl ayalavar cuṟṟattavareṉṟum pārār.
aracar talivāyil kāttal
maṉṉar puṟaṅkaṭi kāttu vaṟitēyām
ennalaṅ kāṇṭumeṉ ṟeḷḷaṟka - paṉṉeṭunāḷ
kāttavi yellāṅ kaṭimuṭipōyk kikoṭuttu
vēttaviyiṉ mikkuc ceyum. 48
48. Say not contemptuously, “What good have we seen in our fruitless attendance at the king’s gate?” The many long days that you have waited, will at last, in turn afford a hand, to rise you to dignity in the Royal Court.
(pa-u) yām maṉṉar puṟaṅkaṭi vaṟitē kāttum - nām aracaruṭiya talivāyili vīṇē kāttu vantōm; e nalam kāṇṭum eṉṟu eḷḷaṟka - evvakiyāṉa palaṉiyum peṟṟilēm eṉṟu ikaḻntu (kavalip paṭā)tiruttal vēṇṭum; palneṭunāḷ kāttavi ellām - mika nīṇṭa kālam kāttavi ellām; kaṭimuṟi pōy ki koṭuttu - muṭiviṟ ceṉṟu utaviyāki; vēntu aviyiṉ mikku ceyum - araca capiyil uyarvik koṭukkum.
(ka-u) araca ūḻiyattāl muṭivil uyarvu uṇṭākum.
nalla karumatti muyaṉṟu ceyyavēṇṭum
uṟuti payappa kaṭipōkā vēṉum
iṟuvari kāṟum muyalpa - iṟumuyirkku
āyuḷ maruntoḻukkal tītaṉṟāl allaṉapōl
āvaṉavum uṇṭu cila. 49
49. Although you may not be certin of the event, persevere to the last in a good undertaking. It is not wrong to drop the medicine of life into the lips of dying men; for there are some things, which are possible, though they are like things that are not.
(pa-u) uṟuti payappa kaṭipōkā ēṉum - nalla kāriyaṅkaḷ muṟṟup peṟutal niccayamalla vāyiṉum; iṟuvari kāṟum muyalpa - (aṟivuṭi yōr) avi muṭivu aṭiyum variyum muyaṟci ceyvar; iṟum uyirkku āyuḷ maruntu oḻukkal tītu aṉṟu - cākum taruṇattiluḷḷa uyirukku uyiri nīṭikkat takka marunti vāyil viṭutal tītalla; allaṉapōl āvaṉavum cila uṇṭu - tīmiyākavum cila muṭivatu uṇṭu.
(ka-u) muṭiyāteṉat tōṉṟiṉālum nalla karumattik kaṭicivariyum muyaṉṟu pārkka vēṇṭum.
ūḻi nampiyirātu muyalavēṇṭum
muyalātu vittu muyaṟṟiṉmi yālē
uyalākā ūḻttiṟatta veṉṉār - mayalāyum
ūṟṟamil tūviḷakkam ūḻuṇmi kāṇṭumeṉ
tēṟṟār eṟikāl mukattu 50
50. The wise will not say, “Though exertion be lid aside, the decrees of fate cannot, on that account, fail”. However ignorant, none will set up a clear light, without any protection in a strong wind, in order to see the reality of destiny.
(pa-u) muyalātu vittu - tām muyaṟci ceyyāmal iruntu; ūḻ tiṟatta - ellām ūḻviṉiyāl uṇṭākum eṉṟu; muyaṟṟu iṉmi yāl uyal ākā eṉṟār - muyaṟci ceyyāmal iruppatāl avi tavaṟippōka māṭṭā eṉṟu (aṟivuṭiyōr) niṉittuk koṇṭirār; ūṟṟam il tū viḷakkam - acivillātu eriyakkūṭiya nalla viḷakki; ūḻ uṇmi kāṇṭum eṉṟu - ūḻviṉiirukkum taṉmiyik kāṇpōmeṉṟu; eṟikāl mukattu mayalāyum ēṟṟār - vīcukiṉṟa kāṟṟukku etirē aṟivu keṭṭum (evarum) koḷuttamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) ellām ūḻāl varum eṉṟu nampiyirātu muyaṟci ceyya vēṇṭum.
ūḻi muyaṟciyāl vellalām
uliyā muyaṟci kaḷikaṇā vūḻiṉ
valicintum vaṉmiyu muṇṭē - ulakaṟiyap
pāṉmuḷi tiṉṟu maṟali uyirkuṭitta
kāṉmuḷiyē cāluṅ kari. 51
51. There is a power, which, with the aid of unyielding exertion, will destroy the strength of destiny. Of this, as is known to all the world, the child, who devoured the germ of fate, and drank the life of Yaman, is an evidence.
(pa-u) uliyā muyaṟci kaḷikaṇ āka - acivillāta muyaṟciyē tuṇiyāka; ūḻiṉ vali cintum vaṉmiyum uṇṭē (tīya) ūḻiṉ valiyik keṭukkum vaṉmiyum uḷḷatākum; pāl muḷi tiṉṟu maṟali uyir kuṭitta kāṉmuḷiyē cālumkari - ūḻviṉiyiṉ muḷiyit tiṉṟu iyamaṉiṉ uyiri uṇṭa kuḻantiyē (mārkkaṇṭēyarē) pōtumāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭām.
(ka-u) muyaṟciyāl ūḻiyum vellalām.
āyvaṉa āyntu ceyka
kāla maṟintāṅ kiṭamaṟintu ceyviṉiyiṉ
mūla maṟintu viḷivaṟintu - mēluntām
cūḻvaṉa cūḻntu tuṇimi valiterintu
āḷviṉi yāḷap paṭum. 52
52. With a knowledge of the proper time and place, and of the root and fruit of the undertaking; after due deliberation; and with a knowledge of the strength of existing resources; an enterprize may be accomplished.
(pa-u) kālam aṟintu - (oru karumattic ceytaṟkēṟṟa) kālatti aṟintu; āṅku iṭam aṟintu - appoḻutē (ceytaṟkēṟṟa) iṭatti yum aṟintu; ceyviṉiyiṉ mūlam aṟintu - ceyyun toḻiliṉ palattiyum (āḻatti) aṟintu; viḷivu aṟintu - peṟum pēṟṟiyum aṟintu; mēlumtām cūḻvaṉa cūḻntu - mēlum tām ārāya vēṇṭi yavikaḷi ārāyntu; tuṇimi vali terintu - tamakkut tuṇi niṟpavariṉ valimiyiyum aṟintu; āḷviṉi āḷappaṭum - piṉṉar tamatu muyaṟciyic ceytal vēṇṭum.
(ka-u) kālam iṭam mutaliyavikaḷi ārāyntu karumattit toṭaṅkavēṇṭum.
karumamē kaṇṇāyiṉār
meyvaruttam pārār pacinōkkār kaṇtuñcār
evvevar tīmiyum mēṟkoḷḷār - cevvi
arumiyum pārarar avamatippuṅ koḷḷār
karumamē kaṇṇā yiṉār. 53
53. They regard not bodily pin; they consider not hunger; they indulge not their eyes in sleep; they esteem not the injury of any; they look not to the rarity of a seasonable opportunity; they care not for contempt; whose eye is fixed on their undertaking.
(pa-u) karumamē kaṇṇāyiṉār - (tamatu) kāriyatti muṭippatilē karuttāyiruppavar; mey varuttam pārār - uṭal varuttattip poruṭpaṭuttār; paci nōkkār - paciyik kavaṉikka māṭṭār; kaṇ tuñcār - kaṇ uṟaṅkamāṭṭār; evar evar tīmiyum mēṟkoḷḷār - yār yār eṉṉa tīmiyic ceytālum poruṭpāṭutta māṭṭār; cevvi arumiyum pārār - kālattiṉ arumiyiyum pārkka māṭṭār; avamatippum koḷḷār - piṟariṉ avamatippiyum karutamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) kāriyatti muṭikkum karuttuṭiyavar etiyum poruṭpaṭuttār.
paki ciṟitāyiṉum kēṭu viḷiyum
ciṟiya pakiyeṉiṉum ōmputal tēṟṟār
peritum piḻipā ṭuṭiyar - niṟikayat
tāḻnīr maṭuviṟ ṟavaḷi kutippiṉum
yāṉi niḻalkāṇ paritu. 54
54. Those, who perceive not, that they ought to beware of even the weakest foe, are greatly in error; if a frog only leap into the deep water of the low pool, it is impossible to see the shadow of an elephant.
(pa-u) paki ciṟiya eṉiṉum - (tamakkuḷḷa) pakikaḷ ciṟiyaṉavā yiṉum; ōmputal tēṟṟār piḻipāṭuṭiyar - (tammip) pātukāttuk koḷḷa aṟiyātavar piḻipāṭuṭiyavarāvar; nīrniṟi āḻ kayattu maṭuvil - nīr niṟinta āḻnta nīrniliyākiya kuḷattil; tavaḷi kutip piṉum yāṉi niḻal kāṇpu aritu - tavaḷi kutittālum (nīr kalaṅkip pōvatāl) yāṉiyiṉ (periya) niḻaltāṉum teriyātu maṟintupōkum.
(ka-u) paki ciṟitāyiṉum kēṭu uṇṭākum.
uṭpaki añcappaṭum
puṟappaki kōṭiyiṉ mikkuṟiṉum añcār
akappaki oṉṟañcik kāppa - aṉittulakum
colloṉṟiṉ yāppār parintōmpik kāppavē
palkāluṅ kāmap paki. 55
55. Those, who fear not the attack of more than ten million avowed enemies, should beware of a single secret foe; the Sages, who bind the whole world by a single word, will guard, with unremitting cution, aginst the enemy, lust.
(pa-u) puṟappaki kōṭiyiṉ mikku uṟiṉum añcār - veḷippaki kōṭikku mēlāka iruppiṉum payappaṭātavar; akappaki oṉṟu añcik kāppar - uṭpaki oṉṟiruntālum (ataṟkup) payantu kāval ceyvar; ulaku aṉittum col oṉṟil yāppār - ulakam muḻuvatiyum oru collāl kaṭṭavalla muṉivar; palkālum kāmappaki purintu ōmpik kāppa - palamuṟiyum kāmamākiya uṭpakiyi mikavum taṭuttuk kāval ceyvar.
(ka-u) puṟap pakivariṉum akappakivar āñcutaṟkuriyar.
vañcakar naṭpi viṭavēṇṭum
puṟanaṭ ṭakamvērppār naccup pakimi
veḷiyiṭṭu vēṟātal vēṇṭum - kaḻiperum
