kaṇṇōṭṭam ceyyēl karuviyiṭ ṭāṟṟuvār
puṇvittu mūṭār potintu. 56
56. After discovering the venomous enmity of those, who burn with inward hate, while they are externally friendly; instantly separate from them; treat them not with too much delicacy; they, who use the knife to cure a sore, will not close it over unhealed.
(pa-u) puṟam naṭṭu akam vērpār naccu pakimi veḷi iṭṭu vēṟātal vēṇṭum - veḷiyē naṭpukkoṇṭu uḷḷē kōpittiruppa varuṭiya nañcupōṉṟa pakiyi veḷiyākki avarip pirital vēṇṭum; kaḻiperum kaṇṇōṭṭam ceyyēl - (avarkaḷiṭattil) mikunta irakkaṅkoḷḷutalākātu; puṇ karuvi iṭṭu āṟṟuvār - puṇṇik kattiyāl aṟuttu āṟṟuvār; potintu mūṭār - ataṉi maṟittu mūṭār.
(ka-u) veḷiyē naṭpuk kāṭṭi uḷḷē paki uṭiyavarukku mukam pārttalākātu (tāṭcaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭalākātu).
vañcaka naṭpu
naṭpiṭik kuyyamvit teyyā viṉicūḻntu
vaṭkār tiṟattarāy niṉṟārkkut - tiṭpamām
nāḷulanta taṉṟē naṭuvaṉ naṭuviṉmi
vāḷā kiṭappaṉ maṟantu 57
57. For those, who, meeting friendship with treachery, secretly devise evil, and array themselves on the side of enemies; surely the appointed day of power has expired; and Yaman must be lying carelessly forgetful of their iniquity.
(pa-u) naṭpu iṭi kuyyam vittu - naṇpariṭattu vañcakam vittu; eyyā viṉi cūḻntu - (avar) aṟiyātapaṭi tīṅku ceyyac camayam pārttu; vaṭkār tiṟattarāy niṉṟārkku - pakivar pakkattavarāy niṟpavarukku; tiṭpam ām nāḷ ulantatu - niccayamāy (avarkaḷiṉ) valiya vāḻnāḷ muṭintatu; naṭuvaṉ naṭuvuiṉmi maṟantu vāḷā kiṭappaṉ - iyamaṉ (avarkaḷiṉ) tīcceyalkaḷi maṟantu cummāvā iruppāṉ? irāṉ (iyamaṉ taṇṭippāṉ eṉṟavāṟu).
(ka-u) naṇpar pōliruntu vañcakam ceypavar maṟumiyil naraka taṇṭaṉi āṭivar.
maṉam kuṟṟāmaṉāl ellām kuṟṟamākat tōṉṟum
maṉatta kaṟuppeṉiṉ nalla ceyiṉum
aṉitteviyun tīyavē yākum - eṉittuṇiyum
tīyavē ceyyiṉum nallavāk kāṇpavē
māciṉ maṉatti ṉavar. 58
58. Where there is a stin on the heart, even good deeds will be all received as evil; but those of spotless minds, though men do them altogether evil, will still look upon it as good.
(pa-u) maṉatta kaṟuppu eṉiṉ - (oruvaṉukku) maṉattil kuṟṟam iruntāl; nalla ceyiṉum - (avaṉukku) nallavaṟṟip piṟar ceytālum; aṉittu eviyum tīyavē ākum - avi ellām tīyaṉavē yākum; mācu il maṉattiṉavar - kuṟṟamillāta maṉattiṉiyuṭiyavarkku; eṉi tuṇiyum tīyavē ceyyiṉum nallavā kāṇpavē - evvaḷavu tīyavaṟṟic ceytālum avar avaṟṟi naṟceykikaḷākavē koḷvar.
(ka-u) piṟar nalla kāriyaṅkaḷit taṉakkuc ceytālum keṭṭa maṉamuṭiyavaṉ avaṟṟit tīmiyāka karutuvaṉ.
nallavar kaṭuñcollum iṉiyatākum
iṉiyava reṉcoliṉum iṉcollē iṉṉār
kaṉiyum moḻiyuṅ kaṭuvē - aṉalkoḷuntum
veṅkāram veyteṉiṉum nōytīrkkummeypoṭippac
ciṅki kuḷirntuṅ kolum. 59
59. Whatever those of kind disposition may say, will still be sweet; but even the honied words of the unkind will be gall. The kindling borax, though it burn, will assuage pin. Arsenic, though it cuses the body to shiver with cold, will kill.
(pa-u) iṉiyavar eṉ coliṉum iṉ collē - nallavar (kaṭiya) coṟkaḷic coṉṉālum iṉiya collēyākum; iṉṉār kaṉiyum moḻiyum kaṭuvē - keṭṭavaruṭiya iṉiya moḻiyum nañcākum; aṉal koḷuntum veṅkāram veytu eṉiṉum nōy tīrkkum - neruppup pōla (ericcalik koṭukkum) veṅkāram erivukoṭuppatāyirunta pōtum nōyi āṟṟum; ciṅki mey poṭippa kuḷirntum kolum - nañcu mayir cilirkkumpaṭi kuḷircciyik koṭuttuk koṉṟuviṭum.
(ka-u) nallavar veṟukkattakka coṟkaḷic coṉṉālum avi iṉiyaṉavākum.
maṉam aḻukku aṟṟiruttalē nalla oḻukkam
poykuṟaḷi vaṉcol payaṉilaveṉ ṟinnāṉkum
eytāmi colliṉ vaḻukkāttu - meyyiṟ
pulamintuṅ kāttu maṉamā cakaṟṟum
nalamaṉṟē nallā ṟeṉal 60
60. That virtue, which, by excluding these four, falsehood, slander, rude words, and unprofitable conversation; avoids offence in speech; and, by guarding the five organs of sense, keeps clear of the pollution of the heart; may indeed by termed the right way.
(pa-u) poy kuṟaḷi vaṉcol payaṉila eṉṟa in nāṉkum eytāmi - poy puṟaṅkūṟal kaṭuñcol payaṉillāc col eṉṉum inta nālum uṇṭākāmal; colliṉ vaḻukāttu - coṟkaḷiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷi nīkki; meyyil pulam aintum kāttu - uṭampil aimpulaṉ kaḷiyum aṭakki; maṉam mācu akaṟṟum nalam aṉṟē nal āṟu eṉal - maṉattil kuṟṟattip pōkkum naṟceyalaṉṟō nallavaḻi eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu.
(ka-u) maṉam aḻukkaṟṟu aimpulaṉkaḷiyum aṭakkiyiruttalē nallavaḻi.
puli pacittālum pullit tiṉṉātu
nallā ṟoḻukkiṉ ṟalitiṉṟār nalkūrntum
allaṉa ceytaṟ koruppaṭār - palpoṟiya
ceṅkaṭ puliyē ṟaṟappacittut tiṉṉāvām
piṅkaṭ puṉattapiṅ kūḻ. 61
61. Those, who have persisted in the course of the path of virtue, though in poverty, will not consent to do evil: the spotted red-eyed tiger, though ravenous with hunger, will not feed on the growing crop in the green meadow.
(pa-u) nal āṟu oḻukkiṉ tali niṉṟār - nalla oḻukka vaḻiyil ciṟantu oḻukupavar; nalkūrntum allaṉa ceyvataṟku oruppaṭār - vaṟumi yaṭintaviṭattum tīyavaṟṟic ceyya niṉikka māṭṭār; pal poṟiya ceṅkaṇ puli ēṟu - palavarikaḷiyum civanta kaṇkaḷiyumuṭiya āṇ puli; aṟa pacittum - mikap pacittatāyiṉum; piṅkaṇ puṉatta piṅ kūḻ tiṉṉāvām - paciya kolliyiluḷḷa paciya payirit tiṉṉātu.
(ka-u) vaṟumi vantālum nallavar takāta ceyalkaḷic ceyya niṉiyār.
oḻukkattip poruḷukku viṟṟalākātu
kulamviṟṟuk koḷḷum veṟukkiyum vāymi
nalamviṟṟuk koḷḷun tiruvum - tavamviṟṟāṅ
kūṉōmpum vāḻvum urimiviṟ ṟuṇpatūum
tāṉōmpik kāttital tali. 62
62. The riches gotten by selling the privileges of descent; the prosperity acquired by selling the benefit of truth; the ease that pampers the body at the expenses of religious duties; and the subsistence derived from selling the ties of friendship and kindred; to beware of all these is chief excellence.
(pa-u) kulam viṟṟu koḷḷum veṟukkiyum - oruvaṉ taṉatu kulatti viṟṟup peṟum celvamum; vāymi nalam viṟṟu koḷḷum tiruvum - meyyiṉāl varum naṉmiyi viṟṟup peṟum celvamum; tavam viṟṟu ūṉ ōmpum vāḻvum; viratattik kiviṭṭut taṉatu uṭali vaḷarkkum vāḻkkiyum - urimi viṟṟu uṇpatum - naṇpar uṟaviṉar mutaliya urimikaḷik kiviṭṭu uṇṭu vāḻtalum; tāṉ ōmpik kāttal tali-tāṉ mikavum kāttal ciṟanta aṟamākum.
(ka-u) kulam mey tavam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum uṟaviṉariyum poruḷukkākak kiviṭutalākātu.
tīyavaḻiyil vanta poruḷ nillātu
iṭiterin taccuṟuttu vañcit teḷiyār
uṭimikoṇ ṭēmāppār celvam - maṭanallār
pommaṉ mulipōṟ paruttiṭiṉum maṟṟavar
nuṇṇiṭipōṟ ṟēyntu viṭum. 63
63. The wealth of those, who exult in having gotten the possessions of the poor, by watching an opportunity to terrify and deceive them; though it increase like the swelling bosom of beautiful women, will quickly diminish like their slender waist.
(pa-u) iṭiterintu - camayam pārttu; accuṟuttu - payamuṟuttal; vañcittu - kapaṭañceytu; eḷiyār uṭimi koṇṭu ēmāppār celvam - eḷiyavarkaḷuṭiya poruḷik kavarntu cerukkaṭi pavaruṭiya celvam; maṭam nallār pommaṉ mulipōl paruttiṭiṉum - aḻakiya peṇkaḷiṉ periya taṉaṅkaḷ pōl (orupōtu) peritā yiruntapōtilum; avar nuṇṇiṭi pōl tēyntuviṭum - (ac celvam) appeṇkaḷiṉ melliya iṭi pōlak kuṟintupōm.
(ka-u) ēḻikaḷi vañcittup peṟṟa poruḷ virivil aḻintu pōm.
uḷḷattil tirupti yaṭiyāmi
peṟṟa ciṟukap peṟāta perituḷḷum
ciṟṟuyirk kākkam aritammā - muṟṟum
varavara vāymaṭuttu valvirāy māya
eritaḻaṉ māyā tirā. 64
64. How impossible is it for mortals to prosper, who, making little of what they have gotten, think much of what they have not; the wild fire, which devours everything as it comes, cannot but die away, as the fuel is consumed.
(pa-u) peṟṟa ciṟuka - tamakkuk kiṭittavi (poruḷ) ciṟitākat tōṉṟa; peṟāta peritu uḷḷum ciṟṟuyirkku - kiṭiyātavaṟṟip peritu virumpum ciṟiya vāḻnāḷuṭiya uyirkaḷukku; ākkam aritu - mēlum celvattāl vaḷarcciyaṭital aritākum; eri taḻal varavara vāy maṭuttu - erikiṉṟa neruppuk kiṭikkak kiṭikka vāyiṭattē koṇṭu; val virāy muṟṟum māya māyātu irā - valiya viṟakukaḷ muṟṟum aḻiya erikiṉṟa taḻal aḻiyātirukkamāṭṭātu.
(ka-u) kiṭittatōṭu tirupti aṭiyātu pērāci koḷvōr peṟṟa poruḷāl aṉupavikkum payaṉiyum iḻappar.
piṟar poruḷi virumpātavarukkup poruḷ cērum
tattam nilikkuṅ kuṭimikkun tappāmē
otta kaṭappāṭṭil tāḷūṉṟi - eyttum
aṟaṅkaṭiyiṟ cellār piṟaṉporuḷum veḵkār
puṟaṅkaṭiya tākum poruḷ. 65
65. To such as do not fail of what is due to their respective station and lineage; exert themselves in their proper calling; and though in difficulties, neither transgress the bounds of virtue, nor cover their neighbour’s wealth; riches will flow to their very gates.
(pa-u) tattam nilikkum kuṭimikkum tappāmē - tattamakkuriya takutikkum kuṭip piṟappukkum tavaṟātu; otta kaṭappāṭṭil tāḷūṉṟi - ēṟṟa muṟiyil muyaṉṟu; eyttum aṟam kaṭiyil cellār - maṟantum pāvavaḻiyil cellāmal; piṟaṉ poruḷum veḵkār - piṟaruṭiya poruḷiyum ācippaṭātavaruṭiya; puṟaṅkaṭiyatu ākum poruḷ - talivāyiliṭattē poruḷ tāṉē vantu cērum.
(ka-u) piṟar poruḷukku ācippaṭātu nallavaḻiyil muyalpavarukkup poruḷ eḷitiṟ kiṭikkum.
uyarntavar celvam palarkkum payaṉpaṭum
potumakaḷē pōlum taliyāyār celvam
kulamakaḷē ēṉiyōr celvam - kalaṉaḻinta
kimmiyār peṇmi nalampōṟ kaṭiyāyār
celvam payaṉpaṭuva til. 66
66. The wealth of the eminently generous, is like a prostitute; the wealth of the middling class is like a chaste married woman; but the wealth of misers, like the charms of unadorned widows, produces no benefit.
(pa-u) taliyāyār celvam potumakaḷē pōlum - talimiyāṉavaruṭiya celvam potumakaḷir pōla ellōrukkum payaṉpaṭuvatākum; ēṉiyōr celvam kulamakaḷē - iṭiyāṉōr celvam kulamakaḷippōlat taṉṉi yuṭiyavaṉukkē payaṉpaṭuva tākum; kaṭiyāyār celvam kalaṉaḻinta kimmiyār peṇmi nalam pōl payaṉpaṭuvatu il - kīḻmakkaḷiṉ celvam āparaṇaṅkaḷik kaḷinta kimpeṇṇip pōla yārkkum payaṉpaṭātu.
(ka-u) nallavariṭattuḷḷa poruḷ ellārukkum payaṉpaṭum; kīḻ makkaḷiṭattuḷḷa poruḷ yārkkum payaṉpaṭātu.
koṭātavaṉ celvattilum vaṟumiyē nallatu
vaḷḷaṉmi yillātāṉ celvattiṉ maṟṟiyōṉ
nalkuravē pōlum naṉinalla - koṉṉē
aruḷilaṉ aṉpilaṉ kaṇṇaṟiyaṉ eṉṟu
palarāl ikaḻap paṭāṉ. 67
67. Even the poverty of the liberal is far better than a miser’s wealth; for the former is not gratuitously subject to the opporbrious reproaches of the world, as ungracious, selfish, and pitiless.
(pa-u) vaḷḷaṉmi illātāṉ celvattiṉ - koṭukkum kuṇamillātavaṉuṭiya celvattik kāṭṭilum; maṟṟiyōṉ nalkuravē naṉinalla - īyum kuṇamuṭiyavaṉiṉ vaṟumiyē mikavum nallatu; (ēṉeṉṟāl); aruḷilaṉ aṉpilaṉ kaṇṇaṟiyaṉ eṉṟu palarāl koṉṉē ikaḻappaṭāṉ - irakkimillātavaṉ aṉpillātavaṉ, mukam pārātavaṉ eṉṟu palarāl vīṇē ikaḻappaṭamāṭṭāṉ.
(ka-u) koṭukkum kuṇamillāta celvari ulakattavar iḻittuk kūṟuvar.
koṭukka iyalāviṭiṉum iṉcol vēṇṭum
īki yariteṉiṉum iṉcoliṉum nalkūrtal
ōō koṭitu koṭitammā - nākoṉṟu
tīviṉik kammiyaṉāl vāyppūṭ ṭiṭappaṭiṉmaṟ
ṟāvā vivareṉcey vār. 68
68. It may be impossible to give; but how cruel is it to be at the same time poor of kind words; yet what else can they do; if, as by a smith, their tongues be pierced through, and their mouths locked up by the evil deeds of a former life?
(pa-u) īki aritu eṉiṉum; koṭuttal muṭiyātāyiṉum; iṉcoliṉum nalkūrtal ōō koṭitu koṭitu ammā - iṉiya col kūṟuvatilum vaṟumi aṭital aiyō mikak koṭiyatākum; tīviṉik kammiyaṉāl nā koṉṟu vāyppūṭṭu iṭappaṭiṉ ivar ā ā eṉ ceyvār - tīviṉi eṉṉum kammāḷaṉāl nāvik kutti vāyppūṭṭup pōṭappaṭṭiruppatāl avarkaḷ vēṟu eṉṉa ceyvārkaḷ.
(ka-u) koṭukkāviṭiṉum iṉcol collavēṇṭum.
col vaṉmiyip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum
colvaṉmi yuṇṭeṉiṟ koṉṉē viṭuttoḻital
nalviṉi kōṟaliṉ vēṟalla - vallittam
ākkaṅ keṭuva tuḷateṉiṉum añcupavō
vākkiṉ payaṉkoḷ pavar. 69
69. Where a man’s words possess influence without cuse to neglect to avil himself of it, is nothing else than to destroy the effect of good works; even when their wealth is threatened with immediate ruin, should those fear, who possess the advantages of eloquence?
(pa-u) colvaṉmi uṇṭu eṉiṉ - oruvaṉukkut taṉatu collukku valimi uṇṭāṉāl; koṉṉē viṭuttu oḻital nalviṉi kōṟaliṉ vēṟalla - (ataṉip payaṉpaṭuttātu) vīṇē viṭṭiruttal nalla kāriyaṅkaḷi aḻittaliṉ vēṟu ākamāṭṭātu; vākkiṉ payaṉkoḷvār valli tam ākkam keṭuvatu uḷatu eṉiṉum añcupavō - colliṉ valimiyāl payaṉaṭiya vallavar virivil tamatu poruḷellām keṭṭup pōvatāyiruntālum ataṟkup payantu tamatu colvaṉmiyip payaṉpaṭuttātu viṭuvarō?
(ka-u) colvaṉmiyuṭiyavar ataṉip payaṉpaṭutti ataṉālām payaṉi aṭital vēṇṭum.
periyōr aṟaṉallā vaḻiyil poruḷīṭṭār
ciṟumuyaṟci ceytāṅ kuṟupayaṉ koḷḷap
peṟumeṉiṟ ṟāḻvarō tāḻār - aṟaṉalla
eṇmiya vāyiṉuṅ kiviṭ ṭariteṉiṉum
ōṇmiyiṟ ṟīrntoḻuka lār. 70
70. When they can procure a great advantage by a little exertion, shall they shrink from it; who abandon what is contrary to virtue though it be easy, to pursue decidedly the path of duty, however difficult?
(pa-u) aṟaṉ alla oṇmiyiṉ tīrntu oḻukalār - taruma mallā vaḻiyil vilakicceṉṟu oḻukātavar; ciṟu muyaṟci ceytu āṅku uṟupayaṉ koḷḷap peṟum eṉiṉ - ciṟiya muyaṟciyic ceytu periya payaṉi aṭiyakkūṭumāṉāl; eṇmiya vāyiṉum kiviṭṭu aritu eṉiṉum tāḻvarō - ceytaṟku eḷimiyuṭiya vāyiṉum aṟattiviṭṭuk kaṭumi yāṉavaṟṟic ceyyat tuṇivarō? tāḻār - tuṇiyār.
(ka-u) ciṟiya muyaṟciyāl periya poruḷ varumāyiṉum aṟaneṟiyil oḻukuvōr avaṟṟic ceyyat tuṇiyār.
ceyya muṭiyātati ceyya muṭiyumeṉak kūṟum puṉmi
ceyyakkaṭava allaṉavuñ ceytumaṉ ṉeṉpār
nayattaku nākarika meṉṉām - ceyirtturippiṉ
neñcunōm eṉṟu talitumippāṉ ṟaṇṇaḷipōl
eñcā teṭutturikkaṟ pāṟṟu. 71
71. Of what avil is the flattering politeness of men, who forwardly profess that they will do things, which are not in their power to do? It may be fitly compared to the tender feelings of a man, who lest harsh words should grieve the heart of another, would cut off his head.
(pa-u) ceya kaṭava allaṉavum ceytumaṉ eṉpār - ceyyataṟ karumiyuṭi kāriyaṅkaḷi mikutiyum ceytu muṭippōm eṉṟu colli niṟpavarkaḷatu; nayattaku nākarikam eṉṉām - virumpattakka mukamaṉ pēccu eṉṉa payaṉic ceyyum; (icceyal etupōleṉil) ceyirttu urippiṉ neñcu nēram eṉṟu - kōpittuc coṉṉāl maṉam varuntumeṉṟu; tali tumippāṉ taṇ aḷipōl eñcātu eṭuttu urikkaṟpāṟṟu - taliyi veṭṭupavaṉatu karuṇi pōṉṟateṉṟu virittuk kūṟattakkatākum.
(ka-u) tāṉ ceyya muṭiyāta karumattic ceyya muṭiyumeṉṟu colpavariṉ ceyal payaṉaṟṟatu.
valiyaṟṟavar payaṉaṟṟaṉa ceyyiṉum āravārippar
allaṉa ceyyiṉum ākulaṅ kūḻākkoṇ
ṭolkātār vāyviṭ ṭulampupa - vallār
piṟarpiṟar ceypapōṟ ceytakka ceytāṅ
kaṟimaṭam pūṇṭuniṟpā rār. 72
72. Many, to reap a harvest of empty sound, scruple not to boast aloud, though they have done nothing; on the contrary, where are the men, who, when they have done a good action, assume feigned ignorance, as if others had done it?
(pa-u) ollātār - valiyaṟṟavar; allaṉa ceyyiṉum ākulam kūḻ ā koṇṭu vāy viṭṭu ulampum - payaṉallātavaṟṟic ceytālum āravārattiyē payaṉākakkoṇṭu vāyviṭṭu āravārañ ceyvar; vallār piṟar - valiya piṟar; piṟar ceypapōl ceytakka ceytu - piṟarāl ceyyappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟa ceyyattakka ceyalkaḷic ceytu; āṅku aṟimaṭam pūṇṭu niṟpār - avviṭattil aṟiyāmiyuṭiyavarpōl aṭakkamāka iruppar.
(ka-u) ariyavaṟṟic ceyya māṭṭātavar aṭakkamiṉṟi ārāvārañ ceytiruppar.
piṟar paḻiyi eṭuttut tūṟṟuvāṉ tiṟam
pakiyiṉṟu pallār paḻiyeṭut tōti
nakiyoṉṟē naṉpayaṉāk koḷvāṉ - payamiṉṟu
meyvitirppuk kāṇpāṉ koṭiṟuṭittuk kolvāṉpōl
kivitirt tañcap paṭum. 73
73. A man who, without malice, takes up, and dwells upon the scandal of others, for the solitary advantage of exciting lughter, will be feared and trembled at, like a man, who, without any benefit to himself, would kill his neighbour by a blow on the cheek, that the might see the body quiver in death.
(pa-u) paki iṉṟu pallār paḻi eṭuttu ōti - oru pakiyum illātirukkavum palarukkuḷḷa vacikaḷi eṭuttut tūṟṟi; naki oṉṟē nal payaṉāk koḷvāṉ - nakiyāṭutaliyē nalla payaṉ eṉṟu koḷpavaṉ; payamiṉṟu meyvitirppuk kāṇpāṉ koṭiṟu uṭittuk kolvāṉpōl añcappaṭum - yātum payaṉillāmal uṭampil uṇṭākum naṭukkattik kāṇutaṟ poruṭṭu piṟaruṭiya kaṉṉatti uṭippavaṉippōla añcat takkavaṉāvāṉ.
(ka-u) pakiyillātirukkavum piṟar paḻikaḷē eṭuttut tūṟṟi nakiyāṭukiṉṟavaṉi añcutal vēṇṭum.
teyvam niṉṟu oṟukkum
teyva muḷateṉpār tīya ceyappukiṉ
teyvamē kaṇṇiṉṟu niṉṟoṟukkum - teyvam
ilateṉpārk killittam iṉputalvark kaṉṟē
palakālum colvār payaṉ. 74
74. If those, who confess the existence of God, enter on an evil action, God will stay and rebuke them without mercy; not those, who say, “there is no God”. Is it not to their own dear children, that men patiently repeat instruction?
(pa-u) teyvam uḷatu eṉpār tīya ceya pukiṉ - teyvam uṇṭu eṉkiṉṟavar tīya ceyalkaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāl; teyvamē kaṇ niṉṟu oṟukkum-atteyvamē kaṇṇukku muṉṉāl niṉṟu taṇṭikkum; teyvam ilatu eṉpārkku illi - teyvam illi eṉṟu collu pavarkku avvāṟu teyvam taṇṭippatilli; iṉ putalvarkku aṉṟē palakālum payaṉ colvār - tamatu iṉiya putalvarkaḷuk kallavō (peṟṟōr) payaṉtarakkūṭiya puttamatikaḷic colluvar.
(ka-u) kaṭavuḷ uṇṭeṉpavar tīyavaḻiyil ceṉṟāl kaṭavuḷ taṇṭittu avari nalla vaḻiyil celuttuvar.
nalvaḻiyil varātaporuḷ tīmi viḷikkum
tīya ceyaṟceyvā rākkam perukiṉum
tīyaṉa tīyaṉavē vēṟalla - tīyaṉa
nallaṉa ākāvām nāviṉ puṟanakkik
kolluṅ kavayamāp pōl. 75
75. Though the prosperity of those who do evil, increase; still evil is but evil; evil will never be good. Like the wild cow, which licks the body with her tongue, so as to create pleasing sensation, while at the same time she thereby cuses death.
(pa-u) tīya ceyal ceyvār ākkam perukiṉum - keṭṭa kāriyaṅ kaḷic ceypavariṉ celvam vaḷarvatāṉālum; tīyaṉa - tīya vaḻiyil vanta accelvaṅkaḷ; nā iṉpuṟa nakki kollum kavayamāpōl - nāviṉāl iṉpam tarumāṟu nakkip piṉ kolkiṉṟa kāṭṭup pacuvip pōla; tīyaṉavē vēṟalla - tīyaviyallāmal vēṟalla; tīyaṉa nallaṉa ākā - tīyavi nallavi ākamāṭṭā.
(ka-u) tīya ceyalāl vantaporuḷ tīmiyiyē viḷikkum.
paḻi nāṇuvār uyarntavarāvar
naṉmakkaḷ cennāt taḻumpirukka nāḷvāyum
cenneṟic celvāriṟ kīḻallar - muṉṉittam
ūḻvali yuṉṉip paḻināṇi yuḷḷuṭivār
tīya ceyiṉuñ cila. 76
76. Not inferior to those who walk every day in the right way, so as to gin the approbation of good men; are the men who though they may do some things wrong, are broken in spirit at the thought of the force of destiny, and with the shame of reproach.
(pa-u) tam muṉṉi ūḻvali uṉṉi - tām muṟpiṟappiṟ ceyta tamatu ūḻiṉ valiyāl (tīviṉi ceyya nērntati) niṉittu; paḻi nāṇi uḷḷuṭivār - (varum) paḻic collukku añci maṉam varuntuvōr; cila tīya ceyiṉum - tīyavi cilavaṟṟic ceytālum; (avarkaḷ) naṉmakkaḷ cem nā taḻumpu irukka nāḷvāyum cenneṟi ceyvāriṉ - nallōr (pala muṟiyum avar naṉmiyi eṭuttuc collutalāl) nāttaḻum pēṟumpaṭi tiṉamum nalloḻukkattil celpavarilum pārkka; kīḻallar - tāḻntavarākamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) tām ceyta kuṟṟattukkāka varuntum iyalpiṉar naṉṉeṟiyil oḻukum uyarntavarukkut tāḻntavarākamāṭṭār.
piṟaṉ maṉi nayattal
piṟaṉvari niṉṟāḷ kaṭittalic cēṟal
aṟaṉaṉṟē āyiṉu māka - ciṟuvariyum
taṉṉalatta tāyiṉuṅ koḷka nalamaṉṟē
meynnaṭuṅka vuṇṇaṭuṅku nōy. 77
77. To visit the gate of a neighbour’s wife is certinly not virtue. But suppose it to be so. Then if it yield even a brief enjoyment, be it allowed; but surely the torture, that shakes the body and the mind with agitation, is not enjoyment.
(pa-u) piṟaṉ vari niṉṟāḷ kaṭittali cēṟal - piṟaṉuṭiya ellikkuḷ niṟpavaḷākiya oruvaṉ maṉiviyiṉ talivāyi liṭattuc cellutal; aṟaṉ aṉṟu - nalla oḻukkamaṉṟu; āyiṉum ākuka - appaṭi nallatu allāviṭiṉum ākuka; ciṟu variyum nalattatu āyiṉ koḷka - ciṟitaḷavāyiṉum iṉpam taruvatāyiṉ ataṉik kikkoḷka; nalam aṉṟē - āṉāl atu iṉpan taruvataṉṟu; mey naṭuṅka maṉam naṭuṅkum nōy - uṭal naṭuṅka maṉamum naṭuṅkat tuṉpan taruvatākum.
(ka-u) piṟaṉ maṉiyāḷi virumputal tuṉpan taruvatākum.
iṉpam tuyttal
karumañ citiyāmē kalvi keṭāmē
tarumamun tāḻvu paṭāmē - perituntam
iṉṉalamuṅ kuṉṟāmē yēriḷaṅ kompaṉṉār
naṉṉalan tuyttal nalam. 78
78. Without interruption to business; without ruining learning without preventing charity; and without greatly diminishing our household goods; the enjoyment of the charms of those who resemble a beautiful young branch, may be considered good.
(pa-u) karumam citiyāmē - tām ceyyum kāriyam keṭāmalum; kalvi keṭāmē - paṭippuk keṭāmalum; tarumamum tāḻvu paṭāmē - tarumamum kuṟivu varāmal; peritum tam ilnalamum kuṉṟāmē - peritum tamatu vīṭṭu naṉmikaḷum keṭāmal; ēr iḷa kompu aṉṉār nal nalam tuyttal tali - aḻakiya iḷam kompiṉi yotta tammaṉiviyariṉ nalla iṉpatti aṉupavittal nallatu.
(ka-u) taṉatu kaṭamikaḷukkup paṅkam varāmal maṉiviyōṭu cērntu iṉpam nukartal vēṇṭum.
kāmattiṉ ceyal
koliyañcāhr poynnāṇār māṉamum ōmpār
kaḷavoṉṟō ēṉiyavuñ ceyvār - paḻiyōṭu
pāvamiḵ teṉṉār piṟituṟ ṟeṉceyyār
kāmaṅ katuvappaṭ ṭār. 79
79. They fear not murder; they are not ashamed of falsehood; they preserve not reputation; they rob not in one way only, but in all; they think not of any act that it is sin attended with infamy; what else will they not do, who have fallen into the power of lust?
(pa-u) kāmam katuvappaṭṭār - kāmattāl kavarappaṭṭavar; koli añcār - koli ceyvataṟkup payappaṭār; poy nāṇār - poy colla veṭkappaṭār; māṉamum ōmpār - tamatu mariyātiyiyum pātukākkamāṭṭār; kaḷavu oṉṟō - kaḷavuceytal oṉṟu māttiramō; ēṉiyavum ceyvār - mēlum pāvaṅkaḷiyum ceyvār; iḵtu paḻiyoṭu pāvam eṉṉār - entat tīya ceyaliyum paḻiyum pāvamumuṭiyatu eṉṟu niṉiyār; piṟitu eṉ ceyyār - vēṟuyātu ceyyamāṭṭār (ellāp pāvaṅkaḷum ceyvar)
(ka-u) kāmattiṉ vacappaṭṭār ellāp pāvaṅkaḷiyum ceyya añcamāṭṭār.
iṉippi virumpātu kippi virumpu miyalpu
tiruviṉu nallāḷ maṉikkiḻatti yēṉum
piṟarmaṉikkē pīṭaḻintu niṟpar - naṟuviya
vāyiṉa vēṉum umiḻntu kaṭuttiṉṉum
tīya vilaṅkiṉ cila. 80
80. There are some, who though their own wife he more beautiful than Lachmi; nevertheless degrade themselves by hankering after their neighbour’s; like an evil beast, which though it has sweet food in its mouth, will reject it to feed on bitter.
(pa-u) maṉikkiḻatti tiruviṉum nallāḷ eṉiṉum - maṉivi tirumakaḷilum aḻakuṭiyavaḷāyiruntālum; naṟuviya vāyiṉa ēṉum umiḻntu - iṉiya poruḷ vāyiliruntaṉavāyiṉum avaṟṟik kakkiviṭṭu; kaṭu tiṉṉum tīya vilaṅkiṉ cilar - kippuḷḷa poruḷit tiṉṉum keṭṭa vilaṅkupōṉṟa cilar; piṟar maṉikkē pīṭu aḻintu niṟpar - piṟaruṭiya maṉiyāḷiṉ cērkkikkē tamatu perumi keṭṭu niṟpar.
(ka-u) tīyavar tamatu maṉiviyar aḻakuṭiyavarāyiruppiṉum piṟa peṇkaḷiyē virumpuvar.
makkaṭpēṟē ciṟanta pēṟu
kaṟpuṭut taṉpu muṭittunāṇ meypūci
naṟkuṇa naṟceyki pūṇṭāṭku - makkaṭpē
ṟeṉpatō rākkamum uṇṭāyiṉ illaṉṟē
koṇṭāṟkuc ceytavam vēṟu. 81
81. If a wife clothed with chastity, crowned with affection, anointed with modesty, and adorned with the jewels of a good heart and life, possess only the wealth of child-bearing; what more can her husband have to seek by the performance of penance?
(pa-u) kaṟpu uṭuttu - kaṟpākiya āṭiyi uṭuttu; aṉpu muṭittu - aṉpākiya pūvic cūṭi; mey nāṇ pūci - uṭampil naṟumaṇamākiya cāntip pūci; naṟkuṇam naṟceyki pūṇṭāṭku - naṟkuṇam naṟceykikaḷi āparaṇamāka aṇintavaḷukku; makkaḷ pēṟu eṉpatōr celvam uṇṭāyiṉ - piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟiruttal eṉṉum celvam uṇṭāṉāl; koṇṭāṟku cey tavam vēṟu il - kaṇavaṉukku vēṟu ceyya vēṇṭiya tavam oṉṟumilli.
(ka-u) naṟkuṇa naṟceykiyuḷḷa maṉivikkup piḷḷip pēṟu iruttal ciṟappu.
mātar maṉam
ēnteḻil mikkāṉ iḷiyāṉ icivallāṉ
kāntiyar kaṇkavar nōkkattāṉ - vāynta
nayaṉuṭi iṉcollāṉ kēḷeṉiṉu mātark
kayalāṉmēl ākum maṉam. 82
82. Though their husband be of surpassing beauty, youthful, powerful in song, of an aspect to ravish the eyes of midens, and uniting truth with courtesy in his pleasing address; the heart of women will still be fixed on others.
(pa-u) kēḷ ēntu eḻil mikkāṉ - kaṇavaṉ ciṟanta aḻakil uyarntavaṉ; iḷiyāṉ - iḷamipparuvamuṭiyavaṉ; icivallāṉ - irākam pāṭuvatil vallavaṉ; kāntiyar kaṇkavar nōkkattāṉ - peṇkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷik kavarum tōṟṟattavaṉ; vāynta nayaṉ uṭi iṉ collāṉ eṉiṉum - poruttamāṉa melliya iṉiya collip pēcukiṉṟavaṉ āṉālum; mātarkku maṉam ayalār mēl ākum - peṇkaḷukku maṉam ayalavarmēl cellum.
(ka-u) kaṇavaṉ aḻaku naṟkuṇam mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟappuṭiyavaṉā yiruppiṉum kaṟpillāta makaḷiriṉ maṉam ayal āṭavar mēṟ cellum.
kaṟpil makaḷir
kaṟpil makaḷiṉ nalamviṟ ṟuṇavukoḷḷum
poṟṟoṭi nallār naṉinallar - maṟṟuttaṅ
kēḷvarkkum ētilarkkum taṅkaṭkum taṅkiḷiñar
yāvarkkum kēṭucū ḻār. 83
83. Far better than a wife without chasity, are the fair, with golden bracelets, who subsist by selling their favours; for they do not involve in misery their husband, their neighbour, themselves and all their connexions.
(pa-u) nalam viṟṟu uṇavu koḷḷum poṉtoṭi nallār - tamatu iṉpattip piṟarukku viṟṟu uṇavu koḷkiṉṟa poṉvaḷi aṇinta peṇkaḷ; kaṟpil makaḷiriṉ naṉi nallar - kaṟpillāta peṇkaḷilum pārkka mika nallavarāvar; tam kēḷvarkkum - (muṉkūṟappaṭṭa vilimātar) tamatu kātalarukkum; ētilarkkum - ayalavarkkum; taṅkaṭkum - taṅkaḷukkum; tam iḷiñar yāvarkkum kēṭu cūḻār - tamatu uṟaviṉar evarukkum kēṭuceyya niṉiyār.
(ka-u) kaṟpillā makaḷiriṉum potu makaḷir nallar.
kaṟpil makaḷir piṟappu
muṟiyum kuṭimiyum pāṉmiyum nōkkār
niṟiyum neṭunāṇum pēṇār - piṟitumoru
peṟṟimi pētimik kuṇṭē perumpāvam
kaṟpiṉ makaḷir piṟappu. 84
84. Regardless of their duty, of their birth and of propriety; insensible to shame, and respectability; what good quality is there compatible with the folly of fril women? The birth of such is the retribution for grievous sin.
(pa-u) muṟiyum - tamakkum maṟṟavarkaḷukkumuḷḷa uṟavu muṟikaḷiyum; kuṭimiyum - piṟar kuṭiyiṉ taṉmiyiyum; pāṉmiyum nōkkār - tamakkum piṟarkkumuḷḷa takutiyiyum pārār; niṟiyum nāṇum pēṇār - maṉa uṟutippāṭṭiyum nāṇattiyum pātukākkamāṭṭār; pētimikku piṟitum oru peṟṟimi uṇṭē - aṟiyāmi koṇṭa peṇkaḷukku iviyallāmal vēṟu kuṇaṅkaḷumuṇṭō? kaṟpu il makaḷir piṟappu perum pāvam - kaṟpillāta peṇkaḷiṉ piṟappup periya pāvamākum.
(ka-u) kaṟpillāta peṇkaḷiṭattil nalla kuṇaṅkaḷ eviyum kāṇappaṭamāṭṭā.
tuṟavikaḷ peṇkaḷip pārār
peṇmi viyavār peyarum eṭuttōtār
kaṇṇoṭu neñcuṟippa nōkkuṟār - paṇṇoṭu
pāṭal cevimaṭār paṇpalla pārāṭṭār
vīṭil pulappaki yiṉār. 85
85. They, who admire not female beauty; who dwell not in conversation on their names; who excite not the eye and heart with licentious gazes; who indulge not the ear in music and song, and commend not what is contrary to decency, are the men who wage successful war with the unrestrined general organs.
(pa-u) vīṭu il pulam pakiyiṉār - aṭakkamuṭiyāta aimpulaṉ kaḷukkup pakivarāyuḷḷa (aimpulaṉkaḷi aṭakkiya) tuṟavikaḷ; peṇmi viyavār - peṇkaḷiṉ nalla taṉmikaḷip pukaḻār; peyarum eṭuttu ōtār - peṇkaḷ eṉṟa peyarit tāṉum eṭuttuc collār; neñcu uṟippa kaṇṇoṭu nōkku uṟār - neñcil avarkaḷiṉ eṇṇam paṭiyum paṭi kaṇṇālum pārār; paṇṇoṭu pāṭal cevi maṭār - iciyōṭu pāṭappaṭum pāṭalkaḷiyum (cevikoṭuttuk) kēḷār; paṇpu alla pārāṭṭār - nalla taṉmikaḷallāta piṟavaṟṟi yum pārāṭṭamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) tuṟavikaḷ peṇkaḷip pārkka māṭṭār; maṟṟum pulaṉkaḷik kuḻappum ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭamāṭṭār.
tuṟavikaḷ uṇṭi curukkuvar
tuyiṟcuviyum tūnallār tōṭcuviyu mellām
ayiṟcuviyi ṉākuvaveṉ ṟeṇṇi - ayiṟcuviyum
pittuṇṇāk koḷpapōṟ koḷpa piṟarcilarpōl
mottuṇā moympi ṉavar. 86
86. They, who, deeming indulgence in the sweets of sleep and the enjoyment of the charms of women of unsullied beauty, to be encouraged by indulgence in the sweets of feasting, take their food abstemiously as an idiot, are possessed of a strength which none may previl aginst.
(pa-u) piṟar cilar pōl mottuṇā moyppiṉavar - (tuṟavika ḷallarāṉa) cilarip pōla (aimpulaṉkaḷāl) aṭipaṭāta valiyiyuṭiya tuṟavikaḷ; tuyil cuviyum - nittiri iṉpamum, tūnallār tōḷ cuviyum ellām - aḻakiya peṇkaḷiṉ tōḷkaḷic cērum cukamum ākiya ellām; ayil cuviyil ākuva eṉṟu eṇṇi - uṇavuc cukattiṉāl uṇṭākiṉṟavi eṉṟu niṉittu; ayil cuviyum pittu uṇākoḷpa pōl koḷpa - uṇavuc cukattiyum pittiyakkāraṉ uṇavu koḷvatu pōl koḷvar.
(ka-u) uṇavu mikutalāl maṟṟa iṉpaṅkaḷil viruppamuṇṭāku meṉṟu niṉintu tuṟaviyar aṟpa uṇavu koḷvar.
tuṟavikaḷ uṭampik kavaṉiyār
aṉpō ṭaruḷuṭiya rēṉum uyirnilimaṟ
ṟeṉpiyakkaṅ kaṇṭum puṟantarār - puṉpulāṟ
poykkuṭil ōmpuvarō pōtattāl tāmvēynta
pukkil kuṭipukutu vār. 87
87. Holy men, though of tender and benevolent disposition, do not turn back, though their bones show through the skin; should they who possess a heavenly mansion, which they have built for themselves by wisdom, seek to preserve a false tabernacle of rankly decaying flesh?
(pa-u) pōtattāl tām vēynta pukkil kuṭipukutuvār - meyaṟiviṉāl tām amitta vīṭṭu ulakil cellum tuṟavikaḷ; puṉ pulāl poy kuṭil ōmpuvarō - iḻinta ūṉ mūṭiya kuṭiciyip pātukāpparō; aṉpōṭu aruḷ uṭiyar ēṉum - avarkaḷ aṉpum irakkamum uṭiyavarkaḷāṉālum; uyir nili eṉpu iyakkaṅ kaṇṭum puṟantarār - uyirkku iruppiṭamāṉa uṭampu (melintu) elumpu aciyak kaṇṭālum; puṟantarār - ataṟku iḷikkamāṭṭār.
(ka-u) vīṭṭu iṉpatti vēṇṭupavar uṭampip pēṇuvatiṟ kavali koḷḷār.
maṟṟiṉpam vēṇṭuvār ciṟṟiṉpam virumpār
ciṟṟiṉpañ ciṉṉīra tāyiṉum aḵtuṟṟār
maṟṟiṉpam yāviyuṅ kiviṭupa - muṟṟuntām
pēriṉpa mākkaṭa lāṭuvār vīḻpavō
pāriṉpap pāḻṅkumpi yil 88
88. Small as is sensual pleasure, its votaries renounce all other enjoyments; shall they who bathe in the great sea of heavenly delight, sink in the mire of earthly indulgence?
(pa-u) ciṟṟiṉpam cil nīratu āyiṉum - ciṟṟiṉpam ciṟu poḻutiya iṉpamāyiṉum; aḵtu uṟṟār - avviṉpattip peṟṟavar; maṟṟiṉpam yāviyum kiviṭupa - maṟṟa ellā iṉpaṅkaḷiyum viṭṭu viṭuvar; muṟṟum pēriṉpamā kaṭal āṭuvār tām - muṟṟāka (eppōtum) pēriṉpa mākiya periya kaṭalil mūḻkukiṉṟavar; pār iṉpa pāḻ kumpiyil vīḻpavō - ulaka iṉpamākiya koṭiya narakil viḻuvarō.
(ka-u) pēriṉpattil mūḻkukiṉṟavar ulaka iṉpaṅkaḷi virumpār.
peṇ cērkki tavirttal
evviṉiya rēṉum iṇiviḻiccoṉ ṟilleṉiṉ
tevvun ticinōkkik kitoḻūum - avviṉi
kātta lilarēl eṉittuṇiya rāyiṉum
tūrttarun tūrppār avar. 89
89. Whatever be the conduct of those, who profess to have renounced the world, if they be only chaste, even their enemies will rise to them their hands in adoration. If however distinguished in other respects, they guard not this virute, even the profligate will not pass them ever uncensured.
(pa-u) evviṉiyarēṉum - (tuṟavikaḷ) vēṟu evvakiyāṉa ceyalic ceypavarāyiruntālum; iṇi viḻiccu oṉṟu il eṉil - peṇ cērkki eṉṉum oṉṟu illātavarāyiṉ; tevvum tici nōkki ki toḻum - (avar) pakivarum avarirukkum ticiyip pārttuk ki yeṭuttu vaṇaṅkuvar; avviṉi kāttal ilarēl - (peṇ cērkki illāmi eṉṉum) antac ceyalik kāttuk koḷḷal illātava rāṉāl; eṉittuṇiyar āyiṉum - avarkaḷ vēṟu evvaḷavu ciṟanta varkaḷāyirunta pōtilum; tūrttarum avar tūrppār - keṭṭavarkaḷum avar mītu paḻi kūṟuvar.
(ka-u) peṇ cērkkiyi virumpātiruttalē tuṟavikaḷukkuc ciṟappu.
naṟceyalkaḷil kālattik kaḻittal vēṇṭum
paraparappi ṉōṭē palapala ceytāṅ
kiravupakal pāḻuk kiṟippa - oruvāṟṟāṉ
nallāṟṟiṉ ūṅkiṟ pataṟik kulikulipa
evvāṟṟāṉ uyvār ivar. 90
90. There are some, who doing various things with bustling haste, will spend day and night in watering the wilderness; but if by any means, one turn them into the good way, they will be immediately startled with alarm. How shall such prosper?
(pa-u) palapala paraparappiṉōṭu ceytu iravu pakal pāḻukku iṟippa - (palar) palavaṟṟi iravupakalāka virintu ceytu kālatti vīṇē kaḻippar; oru āṟṟāṉ - oru vakiyāl; nalāṟṟil ūkkiṉ meypataṟi kuli kulipa - nalla vaḻiyil celuttiṉāl uṭal naṭuṅkip pataṟuvar; ivar evvāṟṟāṉ uyvār - ivarkaḷ evvaḻiyil taḻippārkaḷ.
(ka-u) kālatti vīṇ ceyalkaḷiṟ kaḻikkātu naṟceyalil pōkka vēṇṭum.
iḷamiyil tavam ceyka
iḷiyam mututavam āṟṟutum nōṟṟeṉ
ṟuḷiviṉṟu kaṇpāṭum ūḻē - viḷiviṉṟu
vāḻnāḷ varampuṭimi kāṇparēl kāṇpārum
tāḻāmē nōṟpār tavam. 91
91. To sleep away our days in careless indolence, saying, “We are young, and may perform the duties of devotion in old age,” might be right, if we could see the limit of our lives; but those, who could do so, whould not relax in the performance of religious duties.
(pa-u) viḷivu iṉṟu vāḻnāḷ varampuṭimi kāṇparēl - (iḷamiyil) maraṇam illātiruppatākiya elliyi aṟintiruppā rāyiṉ; iḷiyam - nām (ippōtu) iḷamip paruvattiṉōm; mutu tavam nōṟṟu āṟṟutum eṉṟu - mutumip paruvattil varuntit tavattic ceyvōm eṉṟu niṉittu; uḷivu iṉṟu kaṇpāṭum ūḻē - varuttamillātu kaṇṇuṟaṅkiyiruppatum muṟimiyākum; kāṇpārum - (tamatu vāḻnāḷiṉ) elliyi aṟiyak kūṭiyavarum; tāḻāmē tavam nōṟpār - kālam pōkkāmal tavattic ceyvar.
(ka-u) maraṇam eppōtu varumeṉṟu yārumaṟiyārātaliṉ virintu tavañ ceytal vēṇṭum.
tīya ūḻāl nallaṉavum tīyavām
nallavi ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉum nōṉāmē
allaṉa allavaṟṟiṉ koṇṭuykkum - elli
viyaṉeṟic celvāri āṟalit tuṇpār
celavu piḻittuyppa pōl. 92
92. Though a man may begin to do good, his evil destiny, without suffering it, will turn it into evil: as those who live by plundering on the highway, lead astray the traveller in the dark night.
(pa-u) nallaṉa ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉum ? nalla ceyalkaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉālum; allaṉa - tīya viṉippayaṉ; nōṉāmē allavaṟṟil koṇṭu uykkum - atil tarikka viṭātu ataṉiyum tīya ceyalākki viṭum; elli āṟu alittu uṇpār - iravilē vaḻippaṟi ceytu uṇpavar; viyaṉ neṟi celvāri - periya vaḻiyiṭattē celvōri; celavu piḻittu uyppa pōl - vaḻi tappac ceyvatu pōlākum.
tīya ūḻ uḷḷavar nalla kāriyaṅkaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉālum avi tīya kāriyaṅkaḷāka muṭiyum.
poy vēṭattāṟ payaṉilli
neñcu puṟampāt tuṟantār tavappōrvi
kañcuka maṉṟu piṟitoṉṟē - kañcukam
eppulamuṅ kāvāmē meyppulaṅ kākkumaṟ(ṟu)
ippulamuṅ kāvā titu. 93
93. The cloak of devotion worn by those who have renounced the world externally, is some what different from a garment; for a garment though it guards not the other organs of senses, yet guards the body-the other guards not even this.
(pa-u) neñcu puṟampu ā tuṟantār tavap pōrvi - neñcil tuṟavātu vēṭattil tuṟantavaratu tava vēṭam; kañcukam aṉṟu piṟitu oṉṟē - atu caṭṭi pōṉṟataṉṟu vēṟoṉṟākum; kañcukam ep pulamum kāvāmē mey pulam kākkum - caṭṭi ellāp pulaṉkaḷiyum kāvāviṭṭālum uṭampākiya pulaṉik kākkum; itu ippulamum kāvātu - ittavavēṭam intappulaṉiyum kākkamāṭṭātu.
(ka-u) maṉatil tuṟavātavaṉiṉ tavavēṭam payaṉaṟṟatu.
vañcittu oḻukutal
vañcit toḻukum matiyilikāḷ! yāvariyum
vañcittōm eṉṟu makiḻaṉmiṉ - vañcitta
eṅkum uḷaṉoruvaṉ kāṇuṅkol eṉṟañci
aṅkaṅ kuliva taṟivu. 94
94. Ye fools, who live in the practice of deceit, exult not in the thought that you have succeeded in deceiving all. That is true wisdom, which will tremble at the awful thought, that there is one everywhere present, who will behold all deceit.
(pa-u) vañcittu oḻukum matiyilikāḷ - (piṟarik) kapaṭañ ceytu vāḻum aṟivillātavarkaḷē; yāvariyum vañcittōm eṉṟu makiḻaṉmiṉ - ellāriyum vañcittōm eṉṟu colli makiḻātīrkaḷ; vañcitta - nīvir vañcaṉi ceytati; eṅkum uḷaṉ oruvaṉ kāṇum eṉṟu añci aṅkam kulivatu aṟivu - eṅkum niṟintavaṉākiya iṟivaṉ kāṇpāṉ eṉappayantu uṭal naṭuṅkuvatu aṟivu ākum.
(ka-u) eṅkum niṟinta kaṭavuḷ ellāc ceyalkaḷiyum aṟivārātaliṉ piṟari vañcittōmeṉṟu makiḻavēṇṭām.
maṟittuc ceyyiṉum paḻi veḷippaṭum
maṟivaḻip paṭṭa paḻimoḻi teyvam
paṟiyaṟintāṅ kōṭip parakkum - kaḻimuṭip
puṉpulāl nāṟṟam puṟampotintu mūṭiṉuñ
ceṉṟutikkum cēyār mukattu. 95
95. The infamy of evil done in secret, will spread abroad as though God had proclimed it to the beat of drum. Though the rank stench of putrid flesh be concealed and covered up, it will escape to penetrate the senses of far distant persons.
(pa-u) maṟi vaḻippaṭṭa paḻimoḻi - maṟiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa paḻic ceyalip paṟṟiya (paḻi) moḻi; teyvam paṟi aṟintu āṅku ōṭip parakkum - teyvam paṟi aṭittup parappiyatu pōla appoḻutē virintu paravum; pul pulāl kaḻimuṭi nāṟṟam - iḻinta iṟicciyiṉ mikak keṭṭa nāṟṟam; puṟam potintu mūṭiṉum cēyār mukattu ceṉṟu tikkum - mēlē potintu mūṭi vittālum tūrattiluḷḷavar mukattil ceṉṟu tākkum.
(ka-u) maṟiviṟ ceyyappaṭum paḻic ceyalkaḷ virivil veḷivantuviṭum.
valiyāṟ keṭṭāl uyyār
meliyāṟ viḻiṉum oruvāṟṟāṉ uypa
valiyārmaṟ ṟoṉṟāṉum uyyār - nilitappi
noyya caḻakkeṉa vīḻāvām vīḻiṉum
uyyumāl uyyā piṟa. 96
96. The weak, though they fall, will by some means survive; but the powerful survive not by any means; light things do not fall from their place with a loud crash; but if they should fall, they would be saved; not so the others.
(pa-u) meliyār vīḻiṉum oru āṟṟāṉ uypa - ciṟiyavar nilitappi viḻuntālum oruvāṟu piḻippar; valiyār maṟṟu oṉṟēṉum uyyār - (valiyavar nilitappi viḻuntāl) vēṟu enta vakiyilum piḻikka māṭṭār; noyya nilitappi caḻakkeṉṟu vīḻāvām - pāramillāta poruḷ nili tavaṟi virivil viḻamāṭṭā; vīḻiṉum uyyum - viḻuntālum piḻikkum; piṟa uyyā -(pāramāṉa) piṟaporuḷkaḷ piḻikkamāṭṭā.
ciṟumiyum perumiyum celvam atikāram pōṉṟavikaḷik kuṟikkum.
(ka.u)ciṟiyōr nili tappik kīḻnili aṭiyiṉum piḻippar: mēlōr nili tavaṟik kīḻ nili aṭintāl uyyamāṭṭār.
periyar ciṟiyar ceykikaḷ
iciyāta pōliṉum mēliyōr ceyki 
vaciyākā maṟṟiyōrk kallāl - pacu vēṭṭut
tīyōmpi vāṉvaḻakkaṅ kāṇpāri yoppavē
ūṉōmpi ūṉṟiṉ pavar. 97
97. The conduct of the exalted is not to be reprehended like that of others, although it may not appear priseworthy. For, are those, who after sacrificing a cow, and cherishing the sacred fire, behold the ways of heaven, to be likened to those who eat flesh to pamper their bodies?
(pa-u) mēliyōr ceyki iciyāta pōliṉum - periyōriṉ ceykikaḷ poruttamillātavi pōlak kāṇappaṭṭālum; maṟṟiyōrkku allāl vaciyākā -(avar pōlallāta) ciṟiyōrkku allātu vaciyākamāṭṭātu; pacu vēṭṭu tī ōmpi vāṉ vaḻakkam kāṇpāri oppavē - pacuvip pali koṭuttu tīyi vaḷarttu vāṉulaka iyalpukaḷ kāṇpavari oppavarāvar; ūṉ ōmpi ūṉ tiṉpavar - uṭampip pātukākkum poruṭṭu iṟicciyit tiṉpavar.
vāṉ vaḻakkaṅ kāṇpār eṉpataṟku - maḻi peyyac ceyvār eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟalām. ivvāciriyar uyirkaḷik koṉṟu vēḷvi ceytal kuṟṟattiṉpāṟpaṭāteṉa nampiyiruntār. ik karuttu ellārukkum uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu.
(ka-u) periyār ceyalkaḷum ciṟiyār ceyalkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil ottaṉapōṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum avikaḷiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ vevvēṟaṉa.
periyār taṉmi 
evareva rettiṟattar attiṟatta rāyniṉ
ṟavaravark kāvaṉa kūṟi - evarevarkkum
uppālāy niṟpamaṟ ṟemmuṭiyār tammuṭiyāṉ
eppālum niṟpa teṉa. 98
98. Our spiritual preceptors, who adapt themselves to the disposition of each, whatever it be, and give to everyone suitable counsel, will stand beyond all others, as their Lord is everywhere present.
(pa-u) em uṭiyār - emmi āḷukiṉṟa periyavar; tam uṭiyāṉ eppālum niṟpatu eṉa - tammi āḷukiṉṟa kaṭavuḷ ellār taṉmikaḷukkumēṟpa iruppatupōla; evar evar ettiṟattar attiṟattar āy niṉṟu avar avarkku āvaṉa kūṟi-yār yār ettaṉmiyarāyirunta pōtilum avvattaṉmikkēṟpa niṉṟu ākavēṇṭiyavaṟṟik kūṟi; uppālāy niṟpa - ellārkkum mēlāka niṟpar.
(ka-u) periyōr maṟṟavarkaḷ taṉmikkēṟpa avaravarkkāvaṉa kūṟittām mēlāka niṟpar.
meyyuṇarntār ceyal
meyyuṇarntār poymmēṟpulam pōkkār meyyuṇarcci
kivarutal kaṇṇāp pulaṅkāppār - meyyuṇarntār
kāppē niliyāp paḻi nāṇal nīḷkatavāc 
cērppar niṟittāḻ ceṟittu. 99 
99. They who know the truth, indulge not their senses in the pursuit of lies, and have in view the acquisition of true knowledge; will join to the door post of cution, the long door of the shame of sin, and will secure it with the bolt of steadfastness.
(pa-u) mey uṇarntār - uṇmipporuḷkaḷi uṇarntavar; poy mēl pulam pōkkār - poyyāṉa poruḷkaḷ mītu pulaṉkaḷic celuttār; mey uṇarcci kivarutal kaṇṇāpulam kāppār - mey aṟivu uṇṭātal karuttākap pulaṉkaḷi aṭakkuvār; meyyuṇarntār kāppē niliā - meyyuṇarn tavarkaḷatu (pulaṉkaḷik kākkum) kāppē niliyākavum; paḻi nāṇal ē nīḷ katavā - paḻikku nāṇum kuṇatti nīṇṭa katavākavum; niṟi tāḻ ceṟittucērppar - niṟinta kuṇamākiya tāḻppāḷi iṟukappōṭuvar.
(ka-u) uṇmip poruḷkaḷi aṟintavar pulaṉkaḷi oṭukkuvar.
tuṟavikaḷiṉ iṉpam
kaṟṟut tuṟipōya kātalaṟkuk kaṟpiṉāḷ
peṟṟuk koṭutta talimakaṉ pōl - muṟṟat
tuṟantārkku meyyuṇarviṟ ṟōṉṟuvatē iṉpam
iṟantavellām tuṉpamalā til. 100 
100. As the first born son presented by a virtuous wife, to a husband, who has attined the shore of the sea of knowledge, is the bliss which appears in the contemplation of truth to those who have entirely renounced the world: compared to which all, that is past is but pin.
(pa-u) kaṟṟu tuṟipōya kātalaṟku - kaṟkavēṇṭiyavaṟṟik kaṟṟu muṭitta kaṇavaṉukku; kaṟpiṉāḷ peṟṟu koṭutta talimakaṉ pōl - kaṟpuḷḷa maṉivi peṟṟuk koṭutta mutalmakaṉip pōl; muṟṟa tuṟantārkku meyyuṇarvil tōṉṟuvatē iṉpam - muṟṟākat tuṟavaṭintavarkku uṇmi aṟiviṉāl tōṉṟuvatē iṉpamākum; iṟanta ellām tuṉpam alātu il - aḵtu allāta maṟṟavi ellām tuṉpam allāmal vēṟu illi.
(ka-u) tuṟavikaḷukku meyyuṇarvē iṉpam aḷikkum.
vīṭṭuvaki aṭippavar
kaṟṟāṅ kaṟintaṭaṅkit tītorīi naṉṟāṟṟip
peṟṟatu koṇṭu maṉantiruntip - paṟṟuvatē
paṟṟuvatē paṟṟip paṇiyaṟaniṉ ṟoṉṟuṇarntu
niṟpārē nīṇeṟic ceṉṟār. 101 
101. They, who have studied and attined knowledge; have exercised self-control; have rejected evil and embraced good; have contented their desires with what they have obtined; and thus by laying hold on their object, have superseded the necessity of works; and employ their thoughts in the contemplation of the one object only, walk in the endless path of happiness.
(pa-u) kaṟṟu āṅku aṟintu - nūlkaḷik kaṟṟu avikaḷiṉ uṇmip poruḷi aṟintu; aṭaṅki - aṭakkamāyiruntu; tītu ōrīi - tīmiyi nīkki; naṉṟu āṟṟi - nalla kāriyaṅkaḷic ceytu; peṟṟatu koṇṭu maṉam tirutti - kiṭittavaṟṟik koṇṭu maṉam niṟi vaṭintu; paṟṟuvatē paṟṟuvatē paṟṟi - tām aṭiyavēṇṭiya neṟi kaḷik kikkoṇṭu; paṇi aṟam niṉṟu - vitikkappaṭṭa aṟa vaḻikaḷil niṉṟu; oṉṟu uṇarntu - kaṭavuḷi aṟintu; niṟpārē nīḷ neṟic ceṉṟār - niṟkiṉṟavarē vīṭṭu neṟiyil niṟkiṉṟavarāvar.
(ka-u) nūlkaḷik kaṟṟu ataṉpaṭi oḻukik kaṭavuḷi aṟintavar vīṭṭu ulaki aṭipavarāvar.
aiyan tiripiṉ ṟaḷantut tiyiṟṟeḷintu
meyyuṇarccik kaṇviḻippat tūṅkuvōr - tammuḷē
kāṇpatē kāṭci kaṉavu naṉavākap
pūṇpatē tīrnta poruḷ. 102 
102. The vision of those, who having measured without doubt and error, and ascertined all things by reason, sleep to external things that the eye of true knowledge may be awake, which looks within themselves, and substitutes spiritual, for material subjects, is the final and perfect attinment.
(pa-u) aiyam tiripu iṉṟu aḷantu - cantēkamum māṟupāṭum illāmal ñāyattiṉāl aḷantu; uttiyil teḷintu - maṉatil teḷintu; mey uṇarcci kaṇ viḻippa tūṅkuvōr - meyyaṟivākiya kaṇ viḻit tirukka veḷikkarumaṅkaḷil tūkkam koṇṭiruppavar; tam uḷḷē kāṇ patē kāṭci - tamatu maṉatil kāṇpatē kāṭciyākum; kaṉavu naṉavāka pūṇpatē tīrnta poruḷ - (ñāṉamākiya nittiriyil kāṇum) kaṉavi viḻippu niliyilum kaṇṭu niṟṟalē muṭinta muṭivāṉa poruḷākum.
